   16  #include <algorithm>
   17  #include <cstring>
   18  
   19  #define WINDOW_WIDTH (float)Math::max((int)Renderer::getScreenHeight(), (int)(Renderer::getScreenWidth() * 0.65f))
   20  
   21  // Glyphs from the menu font. The check-circle is what the store and the
   22  // theme installer draw for "installed", so present reads the same here. The
   23  // unlink glyph was already this screen's "missing"; the warning triangle was
   24  // drawn for "INVALID", a status the check never emits, while UNTESTED -- the
   25  // file is there, its hash is not one we know -- got the missing icon. It
   26  // gets the triangle now, because it is a different problem with a different
   27  // fix.
   28  #define PRESENT_ICON  _U("")
   29  #define UNTESTED_ICON _U("")
   30  #define MISSING_ICON  _U("")
   31  
   32  static std::string biosIcon(const std::string& status)
   33  {
   34  	if (status == "MISSING") return MISSING_ICON;
   35  	if (status == "PRESENT") return PRESENT_ICON;
   36  	return UNTESTED_ICON;
   37  }
   38  
   39  void GuiBios::show(Window* window)
   40  {
   41  	// --all: present files too, not only the problems. The launch-time check
   42  	// keeps the default output, which lists problems alone.
   43  	window->pushGui(new GuiLoading<std::vector<BiosSystem>>(window, _("PLEASE WAIT"),
   44  		[](auto gui) { return ApiSystem::getInstance()->getBiosInformations("", true); },
   45  		[window](std::vector<BiosSystem> ra) { window->pushGui(new GuiBios(window, ra)); }));
   46  }
   47  
   48  GuiBios::GuiBios(Window* window, const std::vector<BiosSystem> bioses)
   49  	: GuiComponent(window), mGrid(window, Vector2i(1, 4)), mBackground(window, ":/frame.png")
   50  {
   51  	mBios = bioses;
   52  
   53  	addChild(&mBackground);
   54  	addChild(&mGrid);
   55  
   56  	// Form background
   57  	auto theme = ThemeData::getMenuTheme();
   58  	mBackground.setImagePath(theme->Background.path);
   59  	mBackground.setEdgeColor(theme->Background.color);
   60  	mBackground.setCenterColor(theme->Background.centerColor);
   61  	mBackground.setCornerSize(theme->Background.cornerSize);
   62  	mBackground.setPostProcessShader(theme->Background.menuShader);
   63  
   64  	// Title
   65  	mTitle = std::make_shared<TextComponent>(mWindow, _("BIOS CHECK"), theme->Title.font, theme->Title.color, ALIGN_CENTER);
   66  	mGrid.setEntry(mTitle, Vector2i(0, 0), false, true);
   67  
   68  	// Row 1 held a tab strip -- "Installed systems" / "All" -- that filtered
   69  	// the list by whether EmulationStation had loaded a system, which nobody
   70  	// could tell from the label, and drew as a row of words with an
   71  	// underline too thin to see on a 640-wide panel. One list, ordered by
   72  	// what needs doing, replaces it; the row stays in the grid at no height.
   73  
   74  	// Entries
   75  	mList = std::make_shared<ComponentList>(mWindow);
   76  	mList->setUpdateType(ComponentListFlags::UpdateType::UPDATE_ALWAYS);
   77  	mGrid.setEntry(mList, Vector2i(0, 2), true, true);
   78  
   79  	// Buttons
   80  	std::vector<std::shared_ptr<ButtonComponent>> buttons;
   81  	buttons.push_back(std::make_shared<ButtonComponent>(mWindow, _("REFRESH"), _("refresh"), [&] { refresh(); }));
   82  	buttons.push_back(std::make_shared<ButtonComponent>(mWindow, _("BACK"), _("BACK"), [this] { delete this; }));
   83  
   84  	mButtonGrid = makeButtonGrid(mWindow, buttons);
   85  	mGrid.setEntry(mButtonGrid, Vector2i(0, 3), true, false);
   86  
   87  	mGrid.setUnhandledInputCallback([this](InputConfig* config, Input input) -> bool
   88  		{
   89  			if (config->isMappedLike("down", input)) { mGrid.setCursorTo(mList); mList->setCursorIndex(0); return true; }
   90  			if (config->isMappedLike("up", input)) { mList->setCursorIndex(mList->size() - 1); mGrid.moveCursor(Vector2i(0, 1)); return true; }
   91  			return false;
   92  		});
   93  
   94  	centerWindow();
   95  	loadList();
   96  }
   97  
   98  void GuiBios::onSizeChanged()
   99  {
  100  	GuiComponent::onSizeChanged();
  101  
  102  	mBackground.fitTo(mSize, Vector3f::Zero(), Vector2f(-32, -32));
  103  
  104  	mGrid.setSize(mSize);
  105  
  106  	const float titleHeight = mTitle->getFont()->getLetterHeight();
  107  	const float titleSubtitleSpacing = mSize.y() * 0.03f;
  108  
  109  	mGrid.setRowHeight(0, titleHeight + titleSubtitleSpacing  + (Renderer::getScreenHeight() * 0.05f));
  110  	mGrid.setRowHeight(1, 0.00001f);
  111  	mGrid.setRowHeight(3, mButtonGrid->getSize().y());
  112  }
  113  
  114  void GuiBios::refresh()
  115  {
  116  	mWindow->pushGui(new GuiLoading<std::vector<BiosSystem>>(mWindow, _("PLEASE WAIT"),
  117  		[](auto gui) { return ApiSystem::getInstance()->getBiosInformations("", true); },
  118  		[this](std::vector<BiosSystem> ra) { mBios = ra; loadList(); }));
  119  }
  120  
  121  void GuiBios::loadList()
  122  {
  123  	auto theme = ThemeData::getMenuTheme();
  124  
  125  	int idx = mList->getCursorIndex();
  126  	mList->clear();
  127  
  128  	struct Entry
  129  	{
  130  		BiosSystem  data;
  131  		std::string name;
  132  		bool        hasGames;
  133  		int         missing;
  134  		int         untested;
  135  		int         present;
  136  	};
  137  
  138  	std::vector<Entry> entries;
  139  	for (auto& systemBiosData : mBios)
  140  	{
  141  		Entry e;
  142  		e.data = systemBiosData;
  143  		e.name = Utils::String::proper(systemBiosData.name);
  144  		e.hasGames = false;
  145  		e.missing = e.untested = e.present = 0;
  146  
  147  		// EmulationStation loads a system only when it has games, so being in
  148  		// this list is "this device has games for it".
  149  		for (auto sys : SystemData::sSystemVector)
  150  		{
  151  			if (sys->getName() == systemBiosData.name)
  152  			{
  153  				e.hasGames = true;
  154  				e.name = sys->getFullName();
  155  			}
  156  		}
  157  
  158  		for (auto& f : systemBiosData.bios)
  159  		{
  160  			if (f.status == "MISSING") e.missing++;
  161  			else if (f.status == "PRESENT") e.present++;
  162  			else e.untested++;
  163  		}
  164  
  165  		entries.push_back(e);
  166  	}
  167  
  168  	// What needs doing first: systems with a problem, and among those the
  169  	// ones there are games for, then by how much is missing. Complete
  170  	// systems follow, games first, so the top of the page is the work and
  171  	// the bottom is the reassurance.
  172  	std::stable_sort(entries.begin(), entries.end(), [](const Entry& a, const Entry& b)
  173  	{
  174  		const bool ap = a.missing + a.untested > 0;
  175  		const bool bp = b.missing + b.untested > 0;
  176  		if (ap != bp) return ap;
  177  		if (a.hasGames != b.hasGames) return a.hasGames;
  178  		if (a.missing != b.missing) return a.missing > b.missing;
  179  		if (a.untested != b.untested) return a.untested > b.untested;
  180  		return Utils::String::toUpper(a.name) < Utils::String::toUpper(b.name);
  181  	});
  182  
  183  	if (entries.empty())
  184  	{
  185  		// Only when the check itself produced nothing: with every system
  186  		// listed, an empty page is a failed check, not a clean one.
  187  		auto text = std::make_shared<TextComponent>(mWindow, _("THE BIOS CHECK RETURNED NOTHING"), theme->Text.font, theme->Text.color);
  188  		if (EsLocale::isRTL())
  189  			text->setHorizontalAlignment(Alignment::ALIGN_RIGHT);
  190  
  191  		// A TextComponent measures itself as one line when built and the
  192  		// list then keeps that height while widening it, so give the height
  193  		// back (0) and it wraps at the width it is given.
  194  		float wrapWidth = mList->getSize().x();
  195  		if (wrapWidth <= 0)
  196  			wrapWidth = Renderer::ScreenSettings::fullScreenMenus() ? Renderer::getScreenWidth() : WINDOW_WIDTH;
  197  		text->setMultiLine(TextComponent::MultiLineType::MULTILINE);
  198  		text->setSize(wrapWidth * 0.94f, 0);
  199  
  200  		ComponentListRow row;
  201  		row.addElement(text, true);
  202  		mList->addRow(row);
  203  	}
  204  
  205  	for (auto& e : entries)
  206  	{
  207  		ComponentListRow row;
  208  
  209  		auto icon = std::make_shared<TextComponent>(mWindow);
  210  		icon->setText(e.missing > 0 ? std::string(MISSING_ICON) : e.untested > 0 ? std::string(UNTESTED_ICON) : std::string(PRESENT_ICON));
  211  		icon->setColor(theme->Text.color);
  212  		icon->setFont(theme->Text.font);
  213  		icon->setSize(theme->Text.font->getLetterHeight() * 1.5f, 0);
  214  		row.addElement(icon, false);
  215  
  216  		auto spacer = std::make_shared<GuiComponent>(mWindow);
  217  		spacer->setSize(theme->Text.font->getLetterHeight() * 0.5f, 0);
  218  		row.addElement(spacer, false);
  219  
  220  		const int total = e.missing + e.untested + e.present;
  221  		std::string summary;
  222  		if (e.missing == 0 && e.untested == 0)
  223  			summary = _("ALL PRESENT") + std::string(" (") + std::to_string(total) + ")";
  224  		else
  225  		{
  226  			if (e.missing > 0)
  227  				summary = std::to_string(e.missing) + " " + _("OF") + " " + std::to_string(total) + " " + _("MISSING");
  228  			if (e.untested > 0)
  229  				summary += (summary.empty() ? "" : ", ") + std::to_string(e.untested) + " " + _("NOT A KNOWN VERSION");
  230  		}
  231  
  232  		auto line = std::make_shared<MultiLineMenuEntry>(mWindow, e.name, summary);
  233  		row.addElement(line, true);
  234  
  235  		Window* window = mWindow;
  236  		BiosSystem data = e.data;
  237  		std::string name = e.name;
  238  		row.makeAcceptInputHandler([window, data, name] { GuiBios::openSystem(window, data, name); });
  239  
  240  		mList->addRow(row);
  241  	}
  242  
  243  	if (idx >= 0 && idx < mList->size())
  244  		mList->setCursorIndex(idx);
  245  
  246  	centerWindow();
  247  	// DETAILS comes and goes with the list (getHelpPrompts).
  248  	updateHelpPrompts();
  249  }
  250  
  251  void GuiBios::openSystem(Window* window, const BiosSystem& system, const std::string& displayName)
  252  {
  253  	auto s = new GuiSettings(window, displayName);
  254  
  255  	for (auto& f : system.bios)
  256  	{
  257  		std::string status;
  258  		if (f.status == "MISSING")
  259  			status = _("MISSING");
  260  		else if (f.status == "PRESENT")
  261  			status = _("PRESENT");
  262  		else
  263  			status = _("PRESENT, BUT NOT A KNOWN VERSION");
  264  
  265  		// The expected hash is how somebody finds the right file; a present,
  266  		// matching one has nothing to look up.
  267  		if (f.status != "PRESENT" && !f.md5.empty() && f.md5 != "-")
  268  			status += " - MD5: " + f.md5;
  269  
  270  		// The path as the system expects it, case kept: /storage is
  271  		// case-sensitive, and addWithDescription upper-cases its label, so
  272  		// bios/scph5501.bin read BIOS/SCPH5501.BIN and a file named as the
  273  		// page said still read MISSING (#308 8-es claude F-ES-06). Built by
  274  		// hand, as MANAGE SAVED NETWORKS' rows are for a network's name.
  275  		ComponentListRow row;
  276  		row.addElement(std::make_shared<MultiLineMenuEntry>(window, biosIcon(f.status) + "  " + f.path, status, true), true);
  277  		s->addRow(row);
  278  	}
  279  
  280  	window->pushGui(s);
  281  }
  282  
  283  void GuiBios::centerWindow()
  284  {
  285  	if (Renderer::ScreenSettings::fullScreenMenus())
  286  		setSize(Renderer::getScreenWidth(), Renderer::getScreenHeight());
  287  	else
  288  		setSize(WINDOW_WIDTH, Renderer::getScreenHeight() * 0.875f);
  289  
  290  	setPosition((Renderer::getScreenWidth() - getSize().x()) / 2, (Renderer::getScreenHeight() - getSize().y()) / 2);
  291  }
  292  
  293  bool GuiBios::input(InputConfig* config, Input input)
  294  {
  295  	if (GuiComponent::input(config, input))
  296  		return true;
  297  
  298  	if (input.value != 0 && config->isMappedTo(BUTTON_BACK, input))
  299  	{
  300  		delete this;
  301  		return true;
  302  	}
  303  
  304  	if (config->isMappedTo("start", input) && input.value != 0)
  305  	{
  306  		refresh();
  307  		return true;
  308  	}
  309  
  310  	return false;
  311  }
  312  
  313  std::vector<HelpPrompt> GuiBios::getHelpPrompts()
  314  {
  315  	std::vector<HelpPrompt> prompts;
  316  	// Only when there is a system to open: the THE BIOS CHECK RETURNED
  317  	// NOTHING row has no action, and a prompt that does nothing is worse than
  318  	// none (#308 8-es claude F-ES-28; es-code-traps.md, the help bar).
  319  	if (!mBios.empty())
  320  		prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt(BUTTON_OK, _("DETAILS")));
  321  	prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt(BUTTON_BACK, _("BACK")));
  322  	prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt("start", _("REFRESH")));
  323  	return prompts;
  324  }
```

    8  class TextComponent;
    9  class ComponentList;
   10  
   11  // One page, every system the check knows about, one row each: an icon for
   12  // the worst of its files, the system's name, and what is missing. Systems
   13  // with something to fix come first, and among those the ones this device
   14  // has games for. A press on a system opens its file-by-file list.
   15  class GuiBios : public GuiComponent
   16  {
   17  public:
   18  	static void show(Window* window);
   19  
   20  	bool input(InputConfig* config, Input input) override;
   21  
   22  	virtual std::vector<HelpPrompt> getHelpPrompts() override;
   23  	virtual void onSizeChanged() override;
   24  
   25  protected:
   26  	GuiBios(Window* window, const std::vector<BiosSystem> bioses);
   27  
   28  private:
   29  	static void openSystem(Window* window, const BiosSystem& system, const std::string& displayName);
   30  
   31  	void refresh();
   32  	void loadList();
   33  	void centerWindow();
   34  
   35  	std::vector<BiosSystem> mBios;
   36  
   37  	NinePatchComponent				mBackground;
   38  	ComponentGrid					mGrid;
   39  
   40  	std::shared_ptr<TextComponent>	mTitle;
   41  	std::shared_ptr<ComponentList>	mList;
   42  	std::shared_ptr<ComponentGrid>	mButtonGrid;
   43  };

  503  std::string GuiGameOptions::getCustomCollectionName()
  504  {
  505  	std::string editingSystem = mSystem->getName();
  506  
  507  	// need to check if we're editing the collections bundle, as we will want to edit the selected collection within
  508  	if (editingSystem == CollectionSystemManager::get()->getCustomCollectionsBundle()->getName())
  509  	{
  510  		FileData* file = getGamelist()->getCursor();
  511  		// do we have the cursor on a specific collection?
  512  		if (file->getType() == FOLDER)
  513  			return file->getName();
  514  
  515  		return file->getSystem()->getName();
  516  	}
  517  
  518  	return editingSystem;
  519  }
  520  
  521  void GuiGameOptions::deleteGame(FileData* file)
  522  {
  523  	if (file->getType() != GAME)
  524  		return;
  525  
  526  	auto sourceFile = file->getSourceFileData();
  527  
  528  	auto sys = sourceFile->getSystem();
  529  	if (sys->isGroupChildSystem())
  530  		sys = sys->getParentGroupSystem();
  531  
  532  	CollectionSystemManager::get()->deleteCollectionFiles(sourceFile);
  533  	sourceFile->deleteGameFiles();
  534  
  535  	auto view = ViewController::get()->getGameListView(sys, false);
  536  	if (view != nullptr)
  537  		view.get()->remove(sourceFile);
  538  	else
  539  	{
  540  		sys->getRootFolder()->removeFromVirtualFolders(sourceFile);
  541  		delete sourceFile;
  542  	}
  543  }
  544  
  545  void GuiGameOptions::openMetaDataEd()
  546  {
  547  	if (ThreadedScraper::isRunning() || ThreadedHasher::isRunning())
  548  	{
  549  		mWindow->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(mWindow, _("THIS FUNCTION IS DISABLED WHILE THE SCRAPER IS RUNNING")));
  550  		return;
  551  	}
  552  
  553  	// open metadata editor
  554  	// get the FileData that hosts the original metadata
  555  	FileData* file = mGame;
  556  	ScraperSearchParams p;
  557  	p.game = file;
  558  	p.system = file->getSystem();
  559  
  560  	std::function<void()> deleteBtnFunc = nullptr;
  561  
  562  	if (file->getType() == GAME)
  563  	{
  564  		auto sourceFile = file->getSourceFileData();
  565  		deleteBtnFunc = [sourceFile] { GuiGameOptions::deleteGame(sourceFile); };
  566  	}
  567  
  568  	SystemData* system = file->getSystem();
  569  	if (system->isGroupChildSystem())
  570  		system = system->getParentGroupSystem();
  571  
  572  	mWindow->pushGui(new GuiMetaDataEd(mWindow, &file->getMetadata(), file->getMetadata().getMDD(), p, Utils::FileSystem::getFileName(file->getPath()),
  573  		std::bind(&ViewController::onFileChanged, ViewController::get(), file, FILE_METADATA_CHANGED), deleteBtnFunc, file));
  574  
  575  	close();
  576  }
  577  
  578  bool GuiGameOptions::input(InputConfig* config, Input input)
  579  {
  580  	if ((config->isMappedTo(BUTTON_BACK, input) || config->isMappedTo("select", input)) && input.value)
  581  	{
  582  		close();
  583  		return true;
  584  	}
  585  
  586  	if (mHasAdvancedGameOptions && config->isMappedTo("x", input) && input.value)
  587  	{
  588  		GuiMenu::popGameConfigurationGui(mWindow, mGame);
  589  		close();
  590  		return true;
  591  	}
  592  
  593  	return mMenu.input(config, input);
  594  }

  596  HelpStyle GuiGameOptions::getHelpStyle()
  597  {
  598  	HelpStyle style = HelpStyle();
  599  	style.applyTheme(mSystem->getTheme(), "system");
  600  	return style;
  601  }
  602  
  603  std::vector<HelpPrompt> GuiGameOptions::getHelpPrompts()
  604  {
  605  	auto prompts = mMenu.getHelpPrompts();
  606  	prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt(BUTTON_BACK, _("CLOSE"), [&] { close(); }));
  607  
  608  	if (mHasAdvancedGameOptions)
  609  	{
  610  		prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt("x", _("ADVANCED GAME OPTIONS"), [&] 
  611  		{
  612  			GuiMenu::popGameConfigurationGui(mWindow, mGame);
  613  			close();
  614  		}));
  615  	}
  616  
  617  	return prompts;
  618  }
  619  
  620  IGameListView* GuiGameOptions::getGamelist()
  621  {
  622  	return ViewController::get()->getGameListView(mSystem).get();
  623  }
  624  
  625  void GuiGameOptions::deleteCollection()
  626  {
  627  	if (getCustomCollectionName() == CollectionSystemManager::get()->getCustomCollectionsBundle()->getName())
  628  		return;
  629  
  630  	mWindow->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(mWindow, _("ARE YOU SURE YOU WANT TO DELETE THIS ITEM?"), _("YES"),
  631  		[this]
  632  		{
  633  			std::map<std::string, CollectionSystemData> customCollections = CollectionSystemManager::get()->getCustomCollectionSystems();
  634  			auto customCollection = customCollections.find(getCustomCollectionName());
  635  			if (customCollection == customCollections.cend())
  636  				return;
  637  
  638  			if (CollectionSystemManager::get()->deleteCustomCollection(&customCollection->second))
  639  			{
  640  				mWindow->renderSplashScreen("");
  641  
  642  				CollectionSystemManager::get()->loadEnabledListFromSettings();
  643  				CollectionSystemManager::get()->updateSystemsList();
  644  				ViewController::get()->goToStart();
  645  				ViewController::get()->reloadAll(mWindow);
  646  
  647  				mWindow->closeSplashScreen();			
  648  			}
  649  			delete this;
  650  		}, 
  651  		_("NO"), [this] 
  652  		{
  653  			delete this;
  654  		}));
  655  
  656  	
  657  }
  658  
  659  void GuiGameOptions::close()
  660  {
  661  	delete this;
  662  }
  663  
  664  bool GuiGameOptions::hitTest(int x, int y, Transform4x4f& parentTransform, std::vector<GuiComponent*>* pResult)
  665  {
  666  	if (pResult)
  667  		pResult->push_back(this);
  668  
  669  	GuiComponent::hitTest(x, y, parentTransform, pResult);
  670  	return true;
  671  }
  672  
  673  bool GuiGameOptions::onMouseClick(int button, bool pressed, int x, int y)
  674  {
  675  	if (pressed && button == 1 && !mMenu.isMouseOver())
  676  	{
  677  		close();
  678  		return true;
  679  	}
  680  
  681  	return (button == 1);
  682  }

   19  class ZoomableImageComponent : public ImageComponent
   20  {
   21  public:
   22  	ZoomableImageComponent(Window* window, const std::string& image) : ImageComponent(window, true, false)
   23  	{
   24  		mLocked = false;
   25  		mFirstShow = true;
   26  		mZooming = 0;
   27  		mMoving = Vector2f::Zero();
   28  
   29  		mCheckClipping = false;
   30  
   31  		setIsLinear(true);
   32  		setImage(image, false, MaxSizeInfo(8192, 8192));
   33  
   34  		setPosition(Renderer::getScreenWidth() * 0.5, Renderer::getScreenHeight() * 0.5);
   35  		setOrigin(0.5, 0.5);
   36  		setMaxSize(Renderer::getScreenWidth(), Renderer::getScreenHeight());	
   37  	}
   38  
   39  	void runAnimation()
   40  	{
   41  		auto sz = getSize();
   42  		if (sz.x() > 0 && sz.y() > 0)
   43  		{
   44  			auto fromOrigin = getOrigin();
   45  			auto fromScale = getScale();
   46  			auto toScale = Renderer::getScreenWidth() / sz.x();
   47  			auto toOrigin = fromOrigin;
   48  
   49  			auto imgHeight = sz.y() * toScale;
   50  			if (imgHeight > Renderer::getScreenHeight())
   51  				toOrigin = Vector2f(0.5, Renderer::getScreenHeight() / imgHeight / 2.0);
   52  			else
   53  				toScale = Renderer::getScreenHeight() / sz.y();
   54  
   55  			Animation* infoFadeIn = new LambdaAnimation([this, fromScale, toScale, fromOrigin, toOrigin](float t)
   56  			{
   57  				auto cubic = Math::easeOutCubic(t);
   58  				auto scale = fromScale * (1.0f - cubic) + toScale * cubic;
   59  
   60  				auto originX = fromOrigin.x() * (1.0f - cubic) + toOrigin.x() * cubic;
   61  				auto originY = fromOrigin.y() * (1.0f - cubic) + toOrigin.y() * cubic;
   62  
   63  				setScale(scale);
   64  				setOrigin(originX, originY);
   65  			}, 350);
   66  
   67  			setAnimation(infoFadeIn);
   68  		}
   69  	}
   70  
   71  	std::vector<HelpPrompt> getHelpPrompts()
   72  	{
   73  		std::vector<HelpPrompt> prompts;
   74  		prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt(BUTTON_BACK, _("BACK"), [&] { delete this; }));
   75  		prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt("l", _("ZOOM OUT"), [&] { onMouseWheel(-1); }));
   76  		prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt("r", _("ZOOM IN"), [&] { onMouseWheel(1); }));
   77  		prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt("up/down/left/right", _("MOVE")));
   78  
   79  		return prompts;
   80  	}
   81  
   82  	void update(int deltaTime)
   83  	{
   84  		ImageComponent::update(deltaTime);
   85  
   86  		if (mFirstShow)
   87  		{
   88  			mFirstShow = false;
   89  			runAnimation();
   90  		}
   91  
   92  		if (mZooming != 0)
   93  		{
   94  			float zoomSpeed = deltaTime * 0.0015f;
   95  
   96  			auto scale = getScale();
   97  			scale = scale + mZooming * zoomSpeed;
   98  			if (scale < 0.01)
   99  				scale = 0.01;
  100  
  101  			setScale(scale);
  102  		}
  103  
  104  		if (mMoving.x() != 0 || mMoving.y() != 0)
  105  		{
  106  			float moveSpeed = deltaTime * 0.0003f;
  107  
  108  			auto org = getOrigin();
  109  			org.x() = org.x() + mMoving.x() * moveSpeed;
  110  			org.y() = org.y() + mMoving.y() * moveSpeed;
  111  			setOrigin(org);
  112  		}
  113  	}
  114  
  115  	bool input(InputConfig* config, Input input)
  116  	{
  117  		if (input.value != 0)
  118  		{
  119  			if (!mLocked && (config->isMappedTo(BUTTON_BACK, input) || config->isMappedTo(BUTTON_OK, input)))
  120  			{
  121  				delete this;
  122  				return true;
  123  			}
  124  
  125  			if (config->isMappedLike("LeftShoulder", input))
  126  			{				
  127  				mZooming = -1;
  128  				return true;
  129  			}
  130  			
  131  			if (config->isMappedLike("RightShoulder", input))
  132  			{
  133  				mZooming = 1;
  134  				return true;
  135  			}
  136  						
  137  			if (config->isMappedLike("down", input))
  138  			{
  139  				mMoving.y() = 1;
  140  				return true;
  141  			}
  142  			
  143  			if (config->isMappedLike("up", input))
  144  			{
  145  				mMoving.y() = -1;
  146  				return true;
  147  			}
  148  			
  149  			if (config->isMappedLike("left", input))
  150  			{
  151  				mMoving.x() = -1;
  152  				return true;
  153  			}
  154  			
  155  			if (config->isMappedLike("right", input))
  156  			{
  157  				mMoving.x() = 1;
  158  				return true;
  159  			}
  160  		}	
  161  		else
  162  		{
  163  			if (input.type == InputType::TYPE_HAT)
  164  			{
  165  				mMoving = Vector2f::Zero();
  166  				return true;
  167  			}
  168  
  169  			if (config->isMappedLike("left", input) || config->isMappedLike("right", input))
  170  			{
  171  				mMoving.x() = 0;
  172  				return true;
  173  			}
  174  			
  175  			if (config->isMappedLike("down", input) || config->isMappedLike("up", input))
  176  			{
  177  				mMoving.y() = 0;
  178  				return true;
  179  			}
  180  			
  181  			if (config->isMappedLike("RightShoulder", input) || config->isMappedLike("LeftShoulder", input))
  182  			{
  183  				mZooming = 0;
  184  				return true;
  185  			}
  186  		}
  187  		
  188  		return ImageComponent::input(config, input);
  189  	}

  210  	bool onMouseWheel(int delta)
  211  	{
  212  		auto scale = getScale();
  213  		float zoomSpeed = 0.1f;
  214  
  215  		scale = scale + delta * zoomSpeed;
  216  		if (scale < 0.01)
  217  			scale = 0.01;
  218  
  219  		setScale(scale);

  223  	void onMouseMove(int x, int y)
  224  	{
  225  		if (mPressedPoint.x() != -1 && mPressedPoint.y() != -1 && mWindow->hasMouseCapture(this))
  226  		{
  227  			auto org = getOrigin();
  228  
  229  			auto scale = getScale();
  230  			auto size = getSize();
  231  
  232  			if (size.x() != 0 && size.y() != 0)
  233  			{
  234  				float dx = (mPressedPoint.x() - x) / (float)(size.x() * scale);
  235  				float dy = (mPressedPoint.y() - y) / (float)(size.y() * scale);
  236  
  237  				org.x() = org.x() + dx;
  238  				org.y() = org.y() + dy;
  239  				setOrigin(org);
  240  			}
  241  
  242  			mPressedPoint = Vector2i(x, y);
  243  		}
  244  	}
  245  
  246  	bool onMouseClick(int button, bool pressed, int x, int y)
  247  	{
  248  		if (button == 1)
  249  		{
  250  			if (pressed)
  251  			{
  252  				mPressedPoint = Vector2i(x, y);
  253  				mWindow->setMouseCapture(this);
  254  			}
  255  			else if (mWindow->hasMouseCapture(this))
  256  				mWindow->releaseMouseCapture();
  257  
  258  			return true;
  259  		}
  260  
  261  		return false;
  262  	}
  263  
  264  private:

  339  
  340  void GuiImageViewer::loadPdf(const std::string& imagePath)
  341  {
  342  	Window* window = mWindow;
  343  
  344  	int pages = ApiSystem::getInstance()->getPdfPageCount(imagePath);
  345  	if (pages == 0)
  346  	{
  347  		delete this;
  348  		return;
  349  	}
  350  
  351  #define INITIALPAGES	1
  352  #define PAGESPERTHREAD  1
  353  
  354  	mPdf = imagePath;
  355  	
  356  	for (int i = 0; i < pages; i++)
  357  		mGrid.add("", ":/blank.png", std::to_string(i + 1));
  358  	
  359  	if (pages > INITIALPAGES)
  360  	{
  361  		mPdfThreads = new Utils::ThreadPool("loadPdf", 1);
  362  
  363  		for (int i = INITIALPAGES; i < pages; i += PAGESPERTHREAD)
  364  		{
  365  			mPdfThreads->queueWorkItem([this, imagePath, window, i]
  366  			{
  367  				auto fl = ApiSystem::getInstance()->extractPdfImages(imagePath, i + 1, PAGESPERTHREAD);
  368  				if (fl.size() == 0 || !g_isGuiImageViewerRunning)
  369  					return;
  370  
  371  				window->postToUiThread([this, i, fl]()
  372  				{
  373  					if (!g_isGuiImageViewerRunning)
  374  						return;
  375  
  376  					for (int f = 0; f < fl.size(); f++)
  377  						mGrid.setImage(fl[f], std::to_string(i + 1 + f));					
  378  				});
  379  			});
  380  		}
  381  
  382  		mPdfThreads->start();
  383  	}
  384  	
  385  	window->pushGui(new GuiLoading<std::vector<std::string>>(window, _("Loading..."),
  386  		[window, imagePath](auto gui)
  387  		{		
  388  			return ApiSystem::getInstance()->extractPdfImages(imagePath, 1, INITIALPAGES);
  389  		},
  390  			[this, window, imagePath, pages](std::vector<std::string> fileList)
  391  		{
  392  			if (fileList.size() == 0)
  393  				return;
  394  		
  395  			for (int i = 0; i < fileList.size(); i++)
  396  				mGrid.setImage(fileList[i], std::to_string(i + 1));			
  397  
  398  			window->pushGui(this);
  399  		}));
  400  }
  401  
  402  static std::string _extractZipFile(const std::string& zipFileName, const std::string& fileToExtract)
  403  {
  404  	auto pdfFolder = Utils::FileSystem::getPdfTempPath();
  405  
  406  	Utils::Zip::ZipFile zipFile;
  407  	if (zipFile.load(zipFileName))
  408  	{
  409  		std::string fullPath = Utils::FileSystem::combine(pdfFolder, fileToExtract);
  410  		std::string folder = Utils::FileSystem::getParent(fullPath);
  411  		if (folder != pdfFolder)
  412  			Utils::FileSystem::createDirectory(folder);
  413  
  414  		if (zipFile.extract(fileToExtract, fullPath, true))
  415  			if (Utils::FileSystem::exists(fullPath))
  416  				return fullPath;
  417  	}
  418  
  419  	return "";
  420  }

  423  {

  428  	}

  433  	{
  434  		for (int i = 0; i < images.size(); i++)

  436  
  437  		window->pushGui(this);
  438  	});
  439  }

  441  void GuiImageViewer::loadCbz(const std::string& imagePath)
  442  {
  443  	auto pdfFolder = Utils::FileSystem::getPdfTempPath();
  444  	Utils::FileSystem::createDirectory(pdfFolder);
  445  	Utils::FileSystem::deleteDirectoryFiles(pdfFolder);
  446  
  447  	Window* window = mWindow;
  448  
  449  	std::vector<std::string> files;
  450  	std::vector<std::wstring> filesW;
  451  	
  452  
  453  	try
  454  	{
  455  		Utils::Zip::ZipFile zipFile;
  456  		if (zipFile.load(imagePath))
  457  		{
  458  			for (auto file : zipFile.namelist())
  459  			{
  460  				auto ext = Utils::String::toLower(Utils::FileSystem::getExtension(file));
  461  				if (ext != ".jpg")
  462  					continue;
  463  
  464  				if (Utils::String::startsWith(file, "__"))
  465  					continue;
  466  
  467  				files.push_back(file);
  468  			}
  469  
  470  			std::sort(files.begin(), files.end(), [](std::string a, std::string b) { return Utils::String::toLower(a) < Utils::String::toLower(b); });
  471  		}
  472  	}
  473  	catch (...)
  474  	{
  475  		// Bad zip file
  476  		delete this;
  477  		return;
  478  	}
  479  
  480  	int pages = files.size();
  481  	if (pages == 0)
  482  	{
  483  		delete this;
  484  		return;
  485  	}
  486  
  487  #define INITIALPAGES	1
  488  
  489  	mPdf = imagePath;
  490  
  491  	for (int i = 0; i < pages; i++)
  492  		mGrid.add("", ":/blank.png", std::to_string(i + 1));
  493  
  494  	if (pages > INITIALPAGES)
  495  	{
  496  		mPdfThreads = new Utils::ThreadPool("loadCbz", 1);
  497  
  498  		for (int i = INITIALPAGES; i < pages; i += PAGESPERTHREAD)
  499  		{
  500  			auto fileToExtract = files[i];
  501  			mPdfThreads->queueWorkItem([this, imagePath, fileToExtract, window, i]
  502  			{
  503  				auto localFile = _extractZipFile(imagePath, fileToExtract);
  504  				if (localFile.empty() || !g_isGuiImageViewerRunning)
  505  					return;
  506  
  507  				window->postToUiThread([this, i, localFile]()
  508  				{
  509  					if (!g_isGuiImageViewerRunning)
  510  						return;
  511  
  512  					mGrid.setImage(localFile, std::to_string(i + 1));
  513  				});
  514  			});
  515  		}
  516  
  517  		mPdfThreads->start();
  518  	}
  519  
  520  	window->pushGui(new GuiLoading<std::vector<std::string>>(window, _("Loading..."),
  521  		[window, imagePath, files](auto gui)
  522  		{
  523  			std::vector<std::string> ret;
  524  
  525  			for (size_t i = 0; i < INITIALPAGES && i < files.size(); i++)
  526  			{
  527  				auto fileToExtract = files[i];
  528  
  529  				auto localFile = _extractZipFile(imagePath, fileToExtract);
  530  				if (!localFile.empty())
  531  					ret.push_back(localFile);
  532  			}
  533  
  534  			return ret;
  535  		},
  536  		[this, window, imagePath, pages](std::vector<std::string> fileList)
  537  		{
  538  			if (fileList.size() == 0)
  539  				return;
  540  
  541  			for (size_t i = 0; i < fileList.size(); i++)
  542  				mGrid.setImage(fileList[i], std::to_string(i + 1));
  543  
  544  			window->pushGui(this);
  545  		}
  546  	));
  547  }

  550  GuiImageViewer::~GuiImageViewer()
  551  {
  552  	g_isGuiImageViewerRunning = false;
  553  
  554  	if (mPdfThreads != nullptr)
  555  	{
  556  		mPdfThreads->stop();
  557  		delete mPdfThreads;
  558  	}

  564  bool GuiImageViewer::input(InputConfig* config, Input input)
  565  {
  566  	if (input.value != 0 && config->isMappedTo(BUTTON_OK, input))
  567  	{		
  568  		std::string path = mGrid.getSelected();
  569  		if (!path.empty())
  570  		{			
  571  			if (!mPdf.empty())
  572  			{
  573  				if (Utils::String::toLower(Utils::FileSystem::getExtension(mPdf)) != ".pdf")
  574  				{
  575  					path = mGrid.getImage(path);
  576  					mWindow->pushGui(new ZoomableImageComponent(mWindow, path));
  577  				}
  578  				else
  579  				{
  580  					// path = mGrid.getImage(path);
  581  					int page = mGrid.getCursorIndex() + 1;
  582  
  583  					Window* window = mWindow;
  584  					window->pushGui(new GuiLoading<std::string>(window, _("Loading..."),
  585  						[this, window, path, page](auto gui)
  586  						{
  587  							auto files = ApiSystem::getInstance()->extractPdfImages(mPdf, page, 1, 300);
  588  							if (files.size() == 1)
  589  								return files[0];
  590  
  591  							return path;
  592  						},
  593  						[window](std::string file)
  594  						{
  595  							window->pushGui(new ZoomableImageComponent(window, file));
  596  						})
  597  					);
  598  				}
  599  			}
  600  			else
  601  				mWindow->pushGui(new ZoomableImageComponent(mWindow, path));
  602  		}
  603  
  604  		return true;
  605  	}
  606  
  607  	if (input.value != 0 && config->isMappedTo(BUTTON_BACK, input))
  608  	{		
  609  		delete this;
  610  		return true;
  611  	}
  612  
  613  	return GuiComponent::input(config, input);
  614  }

  658  void GuiImageViewer::showImage(Window* window, const std::string imagePath, bool zoomSingleFile)
  659  {
  660  	if (!Utils::FileSystem::exists(imagePath))
  661  		return;
  662  
  663  	if (Utils::String::toLower(Utils::FileSystem::getExtension(imagePath)) == ".cbz")
  664  	{
  665  		showCbz(window, imagePath);
  666  		return;
  667  	}
  668  
  669  	if (Utils::String::toLower(Utils::FileSystem::getExtension(imagePath)) == ".pdf") 
  670  	{
  671  		showPdf(window, imagePath);
  672  		return;
  673  	}
  674  
  675  	if (zoomSingleFile)
  676  	{
  677  		// A screenshot is shown as the game was (fork #243, #245).
  678  		auto* image = new ZoomableImageComponent(window, imagePath);
  679  		const DisplayAspect::Transform t = DisplayAspect::forScreenshotPath(imagePath);
  680  		image->setDisplayAspect(t.aspect);
  681  		image->setDisplayRotation(t.turns);
  682  		window->pushGui(image);
  683  		return;
  684  	}
  685  
  686  	auto imgViewer = new GuiImageViewer(window, false);
  687  	imgViewer->add(imagePath);
  688  	imgViewer->setCursor(imagePath);
  689  	window->pushGui(imgViewer);
  690  }
  691  
  692  void GuiImageViewer::showPdf(Window* window, const std::string imagePath)
  693  {
  694  	auto imgViewer = new GuiImageViewer(window, true);	
  695  
  696  	std::string ext = Utils::FileSystem::getExtension(imagePath);
  697  	if (ext == ".cbz")
  698  		imgViewer->loadCbz(imagePath);
  699  	else
  700  		imgViewer->loadPdf(imagePath);	
  701  }
  702  
  703  void GuiImageViewer::showImages(Window* window, std::vector<std::string>& images)
  704  {
  705  	auto imgViewer = new GuiImageViewer(window, false);
  706  	imgViewer->loadImages(images);
  707  }
  708  
  709  void GuiImageViewer::showCbz(Window* window, const std::string imagePath)
  710  {
  711  	auto imgViewer = new GuiImageViewer(window, true);
  712  	imgViewer->loadCbz(imagePath);
  713  }

   19  // Every writer of the saves tree is gated by the transfer lock (D-CLOUD-053),
   20  // and the interface's own sync card is not the only holder of it: a
   21  // cloud_backup started from a shell, a restore or a content run holds
   22  // /var/run/cloud_sync.lock for its whole length with no card on screen
   23  // (#307 PL-068). So DELETE and COPY ask both -- the card first, for its
   24  // words, which point at the card; then the lock, whose words cannot. "" when
   25  // the saves tree is free; else what the refusal says.
   26  static std::string savesTreeBusy(const std::string& cardWords)
   27  {
   28  	if (ThreadedCloudSync::isRunning())
   29  		return cardWords;
   30  	// Not while it is this process's own: a deletion holds the lock for its
   31  	// moment (SaveStateBookkeeper, runDelete), and a press then is queued
   32  	// behind it, not refused as a transfer.
   33  	if (Utils::AtomicFile::isFlockHeld("/var/run/cloud_sync.lock") && !SaveStateBookkeeper::holdsTransferLock())
   34  		return _("A SYNC IS ALREADY RUNNING.") + std::string("\n\n") + _("WAIT FOR IT TO FINISH, THEN TRY AGAIN.");
   35  	return "";
   36  }

   41  // GAME / START NEW AUTO SAVE ending in "..." (#27); at 0.50 two lines left
   42  // the thumbnail 74 px tall there.

   63  	mTitle = std::make_shared<TextComponent>(mWindow, _("SAVE STATE MANAGER"), theme->Title.font, theme->Title.color, ALIGN_CENTER);

   66  	mGrid = std::make_shared<ImageGridComponent<SaveStateItem>>(mWindow);

   68  	// NES and the SNES is not the screen's shape (fork #243, D-UI-080): the
   69  	// tiles are drawn at the system's aspect, the picture scaled to it.
   70  	{
   71  		// A tile that shows one of this game's captures takes the game's
   72  		// display transform -- its system's aspect and the turn the display
   73  		// gave its frame (fork #243, #245). Only that tile: START NEW GAME
   74  		// and START NEW AUTO SAVE carry the arrow, and a grid-wide transform
   75  		// turned and fitted the arrow with the rest (fork #250).
   76  		const DisplayAspect::Transform t = DisplayAspect::forGame(game);
   77  		mGrid->setTileDecorator([t](GridTileComponent* tile, const SaveStateItem& item)
   78  		{

   90  	float screenProportion = (float)Renderer::getScreenWidth() / (float)Renderer::getScreenHeight();
   91  
   92  	float sh = (float)Math::min(Renderer::getScreenHeight(), Renderer::getScreenWidth());
   93  	sh = (float) theme->TextSmall.font->getSize() / sh;
   94  
   95  	// The label's share of a tile is whatever two lines of the tile's font
   96  	// are of the tile's height, never less than the 0.30 it was nor more than half (#27). The
   97  	// tile is the grid's one row: the sheet less its spacing, title and help
   98  	// rows, laid out by onSizeChanged with the same arithmetic. TextComponent
   99  	// wraps on its own once its height clears 1.8 lines, so two full lines
  100  	// make START NEW GAME, START NEW AUTO SAVE and a slot's number-and-date
  101  	// wrap where they used to end in "...". The strings themselves are
  102  	// untouched: they are msgids in every locale.
  103  	// Font::getHeight is the tallest glyph rasterised SO FAR, so measured
  104  	// before the labels have drawn it under-reports: at 640x480 the share
  105  	// computed from it fell to the 0.30 floor while the renderer, measuring
  106  	// later with more glyphs loaded, found the area under its two-line
  107  	// threshold and ellipsised every label (878ec8863b, 2026-09-13).
  108  	// Rasterise the printable range first, so this height is the one the
  109  	// labels are laid out with; and the tile text is told to wrap outright
  110  	// (<multiLine>true</multiLine>) rather than left to that threshold.
  111  	std::string ascii;
  112  	for (int c = 32; c < 127; c++)
  113  		ascii.push_back((char)c);
  114  	theme->TextSmall.font->sizeText(ascii);
  115  
  116  	// The widest line a tile shows is a slot's date and time. A tile is the
  117  	// grid's width over the columns it lays out (the same arithmetic as
  118  	// ImageGridComponent::calcGridDimension: the sheet less its two 0.01
  119  	// side columns, less a margin between columns), and on a 640x480 panel
  120  	// that is about 152 px while the date in the small font is about 150:
  121  	// the labels of neighbouring tiles ran into each other ("21:4709/12/
  122  	// 2026", 02f368914e). Where the date would take more than 0.86 of the
  123  	// tile, the label font shrinks so it fits with a gap either side; the
  124  	// two-line height below is then measured on that smaller font.
  125  	const int columns = (int)(slots * screenProportion / cellProportion);
  126  	const float marginX = 0.01f * (float)Renderer::getScreenWidth();
  127  	const float tileWidth = ((float)Renderer::getScreenWidth() * 0.98f - marginX * (columns - 1)) / (float)Math::max(1, columns);
  128  	//
  129  	// The font a tile ends up with is not the theme's object: the tile makes
  130  	// its own from the size it is handed, through Font::get with the screen's
  131  	// font scale (1.31 under 720 px), and the theme's small font was ALREADY
  132  	// made with the menu scale. Handing the theme font's size straight
  133  	// through -- what this page did from the start -- scaled it twice: on a
  134  	// 640x480 panel a 16 px small font became 20, then 26, which is half of
  135  	// why the labels never fit and why a date measured on the theme's font
  136  	// read narrower than the tile drew it (5776ede212). Ask for the size that
  137  	// comes out equal to the theme's, and measure with the font the tile
  138  	// will use. At 1280x800 the scale is 1 and nothing changes.
  139  	const float minSide = (float)Math::min(Renderer::getScreenHeight(), Renderer::getScreenWidth());
  140  	const float screenScale = Renderer::ScreenSettings::fontScale() > 0.0f ? Renderer::ScreenSettings::fontScale() : 1.0f;
  141  	//
  142  	// One point under the theme's small font (maintainer, 2026-09-15, on
  143  	// the RG SP with RC-12: "the save state manager looks great, but the
  144  	// text could be a tiny bit smaller, maybe one point or so"): 15 px
  145  	// where the 640x480 panel's small font is 16, the date-width shrink
  146  	// below still applying after it.
  147  	const int pointsUnderSmall = 1;
  148  	int requested = Math::max(1, (int)((float)theme->TextSmall.font->getSize() / screenScale + 0.5f) - pointsUnderSmall);
  149  	std::shared_ptr<Font> labelFont = Font::get(requested, theme->TextSmall.font->getPath());
  150  	labelFont->sizeText(ascii);
  151  	{
  152  		// now()'s text is as long as any tile's: the locale's date is one
  153  		// width, and the time half writes the same number of characters at
  154  		// every hour on either clock, AM/PM included (fork #195; TimeText's
  155  		// test holds it to that), so the sample measures the row's shape
  156  		// whatever the switch says when the manager opens. A tile's second
  157  		// line is TODAY at 17:07, YESTERDAY at 14:03 or 09/24/26 at 14:03
  158  		// (fork #195, D-UI-089), so the sample is the widest of the three
  159  		// words with the same "at" and time -- in French AUJOURD'HUI is the
  160  		// longest, in English the date is.
  161  		const std::string at = " " + _("at") + " " + Utils::Time::DateTime::localClockText(Utils::Time::DateTime::now().getTimeStruct());
  162  		const std::string candidates[] = { _("TODAY") + at, _("YESTERDAY") + at, Utils::Time::DateTime::now().toShortLocalDateString() + at };
  163  		std::string widest = candidates[0];
  164  		for (const std::string& c : candidates)
  165  			if (labelFont->sizeText(c).x() > labelFont->sizeText(widest).x())
  166  				widest = c;
  167  		const float widestPx = labelFont->sizeText(widest).x();
  168  		if (widestPx > tileWidth * 0.86f && widestPx > 0)
  169  		{
  170  			requested = Math::max(1, (int)((float)requested * tileWidth * 0.86f / widestPx));
  171  			labelFont = Font::get(requested, theme->TextSmall.font->getPath());
  172  			labelFont->sizeText(ascii);
  173  		}
  174  	}
  175  	sh = (float)requested / minSide;
  176  	const float sheetHeight = WINDOW_HEIGHT;
  177  	const float titlePerc = theme->Title.font->getHeight(2.0f) / sheetHeight;
  178  	// The same style onSizeChanged measures, so the two agree (see
  179  	// helpRowPerc); the debug line is how that is checked in es_log.txt.
  180  	const float helpPerc = helpRowPerc(sheetHeight, getHelpStyle());
  181  	LOG(LogDebug) << "GuiSaveState: help row " << helpPerc << " of a " << sheetHeight << " px sheet (constructor)";
  182  	const float gridHeight = sheetHeight * (1.0f - 0.02f - titlePerc - 0.02f - helpPerc);
  183  	const float twoLines = 2.0f * labelFont->getHeight(1.5f) + 4.0f;
  184  	float labelPerc = gridHeight > 0 ? twoLines / gridHeight : 0.30f;
  185  	labelPerc = Math::max(0.30f, Math::min(0.50f, labelPerc));
  186  
  187  	std::string xml =
  188  		"<theme defaultView=\"Tiles\">"
  189  		"<formatVersion>7</formatVersion>"
  190  		"<view name = \"grid\">"
  191  		"<imagegrid name=\"gamegrid\">"
  192  		"  <margin>0.01 0.02</margin>"
  193  		"  <padding>0 0</padding>"
  194  		"  <scrollDirection>horizontal</scrollDirection>"
  195  		"  <autoLayout>" + std::to_string(slots * screenProportion / cellProportion) +" 1</autoLayout>"
  196  		"  <autoLayoutSelectedZoom>1</autoLayoutSelectedZoom>"
  197  		"  <animateSelection>false</animateSelection>"
  198  		"  <centerSelection>false</centerSelection>"		
  199  		"</imagegrid>"		
  200  		"<gridtile name=\"default\">"
  201  		"  <backgroundColor>FFFFFF00</backgroundColor>"
  202  		"  <padding>8 8</padding>"
  203  		"  <imageColor>FFFFFFFF</imageColor>"
  204  		"</gridtile>"		
  205  		"<gridtile name=\"selected\">"
  206  		"  <backgroundColor>" + Utils::String::toHexString(theme->Text.selectorColor) + "</backgroundColor>"
  207  		"</gridtile>"
  208  		"<text name=\"gridtile\">"
  209  		"  <color>" + Utils::String::toHexString(theme->Text.color) + "</color>"
  210  		"  <backgroundColor>00000000</backgroundColor>"
  211  		"  <fontPath>" + theme->Text.font->getPath() +"</fontPath>"
  212  		"  <fontSize>" + std::to_string(sh) + "</fontSize>"
  213  		"  <alignment>center</alignment>"
  214  		"  <singleLineScroll>false</singleLineScroll>"
  215  		"  <multiLine>true</multiLine>"
  216  		"  <size>1 " + std::to_string(labelPerc) + "</size>"
  217  		"</text>"
  218  		"<text name=\"gridtile:selected\">"
  219  		"  <color>" + Utils::String::toHexString(theme->Text.selectedColor) + "</color>"
  220  		"</text>"
  221  		"<image name=\"gridtile.image\">"
  222  		"  <linearSmooth>true</linearSmooth>"		
  223  		"  <color>D0D0D0D0</color>"
  224  		"  <roundCorners>0.02</roundCorners>"		
  225  		"</image>"
  226  		"<image name=\"gridtile.image:selected\">"
  227  		"  <color>FFFFFFFF</color>"
  228  		"</image>"
  229  		"</view>"
  230  		"</theme>";
  231  
  232  	mTheme = std::shared_ptr<ThemeData>(new ThemeData());
  233  	std::map<std::string, std::string> emptyMap;
  234  	mTheme->loadFile("imageviewer", emptyMap, xml, false);
  235  
  236  	//mGrid->setSize(Renderer::getScreenWidth(), Renderer::getScreenHeight());
  237  	mGrid->applyTheme(mTheme, "grid", "gamegrid", 0);
  238  	mGrid->setCursorChangedCallback([&](const CursorState& /*state*/) { updateHelpPrompts(); });
  239  
  240  	mDeletionsSeen = SaveStateBookkeeper::completed();
  241  	loadGrid();
  242  	centerWindow();
  243  }
  244  
  245  void GuiSaveState::loadGrid()
  246  {
  247  	mGrid->clear();
  248  	mGrid->onSizeChanged(); // To Rebuild tiles
  249  
  250  	bool supportsIncrementalSaveStates = SystemConf::getIncrementalSaveStates();
  251  	bool incrementalSaveStates = supportsIncrementalSaveStates && mRepository->supportsIncrementalSaveStates();
  252  
  253  	auto states = mRepository->getSaveStates(mGame);
  254  
  255  	// A slot the player has just deleted is gone from the grid the same frame;
  256  	// its file follows on the worker, retire first (D-UI-073). Until then the
  257  	// repository still lists it, so it is dropped here rather than shown as a
  258  	// tile that would come back for a second and vanish again.
  259  	states.erase(std::remove_if(states.begin(), states.end(),
  260  		[](const SaveState* x) { return SaveStateBookkeeper::isPending(x->fileName); }), states.end());
  261  
  262  	// What this build shows, for update() to compare the disk against.
  263  	mShown.clear();
  264  	for (auto state : states)
  265  		mShown.push_back(state->fileName);
  266  	std::sort(mShown.begin(), mShown.end());
  267  	
  268  	std::sort(states.begin(), states.end(), [&, supportsIncrementalSaveStates, incrementalSaveStates](const SaveState* file1, const SaveState* file2)
  269  		{ 
  270  			// Show active emulator first
  271  			if (file1->config != nullptr && file2->config != nullptr && !file1->config->equals(file2->config))
  272  				return file1->config->isActiveConfig(mGame);
  273  			
  274  			if (supportsIncrementalSaveStates && file1->config != nullptr ? file1->config->incremental : incrementalSaveStates)
  275  				return file1->creationDate >= file2->creationDate;
  276  
  277  			return file1->slot < file2->slot; 
  278  		});
  279  
  280  
  281  	mGrid->add(_("START NEW GAME"), ":/freeslot.svg", SaveStateItem(mRepository->getDefaultNewGameSaveState()));
  282  
  283  	if (mRepository->supportsAutoSave() && mGame->getCurrentGameSetting("autosave") == "1")
  284  	{
  285  		auto autoSave = std::find_if(states.cbegin(), states.cend(), [](SaveState* x) { return x->slot == -1; });
  286  		if (autoSave == states.cend())
  287  			mGrid->add(_("START NEW AUTO SAVE"), ":/freeslot.svg", SaveStateItem(mRepository->getDefaultAutoSaveSaveState()));
  288  	}
  289  
  290  	for (auto item : states)
  291  	{
  292  		std::string coreinfo;
  293  		
  294  		if (item->config != nullptr && !item->config->emulator.empty() && !item->config->isActiveConfig(mGame))
  295  		{
  296  			if (item->config->core.empty() || item->config->emulator == item->config->core)
  297  				coreinfo = std::string("\r\n") + item->config->emulator;
  298  			else
  299  				coreinfo = std::string("\r\n") + item->config->emulator + ": " + item->config->core;
  300  		}
  301  
  302  		if (item->slot == -1)
  303  			mGrid->add(_("AUTO SAVE") + std::string("\r\n") + item->creationDate.toRelativeLocalTimeString(_("TODAY"), _("YESTERDAY"), _("at")) + coreinfo, item->getScreenShot(), SaveStateItem(item));
  304  		else if (supportsIncrementalSaveStates && item->config != nullptr ? item->config->incremental : incrementalSaveStates)
  305  			mGrid->add(item->creationDate.toRelativeLocalTimeString(_("TODAY"), _("YESTERDAY"), _("at")) + coreinfo, item->getScreenShot(), SaveStateItem(item));
  306  		else 
  307  			mGrid->add(_("SLOT") + std::string(" ") + std::to_string(item->slot) + std::string("\r\n") + item->creationDate.toRelativeLocalTimeString(_("TODAY"), _("YESTERDAY"), _("at")) + coreinfo, item->getScreenShot(), SaveStateItem(item));
  308  	}
  309  
  310  	// The help bar follows the cursor, and a rebuild moves the cursor
  311  	// without a cursor event: after the last slot was deleted the bar still
  312  	// offered DELETE and COPY TO FREE SLOT over START NEW GAME (#93). Read
  313  	// the prompts of whatever is under the cursor now. A no-op before the
  314  	// page is on screen (updateHelpPrompts acts only on the top page), so
  315  	// the constructor's call costs nothing.
  316  	updateHelpPrompts();
  317  }
  318  
  319  std::vector<std::string> GuiSaveState::filesOnDisk()
  320  {
  321  	// The same list loadGrid() builds from: the repository hands out nothing
  322  	// whose file is gone (a8e274598), and what is queued for deletion is
  323  	// hidden until it is.
  324  	std::vector<std::string> files;
  325  	for (auto state : mRepository->getSaveStates(mGame))
  326  		if (!SaveStateBookkeeper::isPending(state->fileName))
  327  			files.push_back(state->fileName);
  328  	std::sort(files.begin(), files.end());
  329  	return files;
  330  }
  331  
  332  // The help row's share of a sheet of the given height -- shared by the
  333  // layout and by the label arithmetic in the constructor, so the two agree.
  334  // The style is the caller's, and both pass getHelpStyle(): the one Window
  335  // draws the bar with (the default theme's helpsystem), which needs nothing
  336  // of this page. It used to be built here from mTheme, which the
  337  // constructor has not made yet when it needs this number, behind a null
  338  // guard that let the two calls measure different styles without a word
  339  // (#151 PL-17). Both callers log the result at debug level, so the
  340  // acceptance -- equal values at 640x480 and 1280x800 -- reads straight
  341  // out of es_log.txt with --debug.
  342  float GuiSaveState::helpRowPerc(float sheetHeight, const HelpStyle& help)
  343  {
  344  	float helpSize = 0.02;
  345  
  346  	if (Settings::getInstance()->getBool("ShowHelpPrompts"))
  347  	{
  348  		const float height = Math::round(help.font->getLetterHeight() * 1.25f);
  349  
  350  		float helpBottom = help.position.y() + (height * mOrigin.y());
  351  		float helpBottomSpace = 0; // Renderer::getScreenHeight() - helpBottom;
  352  
  353  		float helpTop = help.position.y() - (height * mOrigin.y()); // +height / 2;
  354  		
  355  		helpSize = helpTop;
  356  		helpSize = Renderer::getScreenHeight() - helpSize + helpBottomSpace;
  357  		helpSize = helpSize / sheetHeight + 0.06;
  358  	}
  359  
  360  	return helpSize;
  361  }
  362  
  363  void GuiSaveState::onSizeChanged()
  364  {	
  365  	GuiComponent::onSizeChanged();
  366  
  367  	float helpSize = helpRowPerc(mSize.y(), getHelpStyle());
  368  	LOG(LogDebug) << "GuiSaveState: help row " << helpSize << " of a " << mSize.y() << " px sheet (onSizeChanged)";
  369  
  370  	mBackground.fitTo(mSize, Vector3f::Zero(), Vector2f(-32, -32));
  371  
  372  	mLayout.setColWidthPerc(0, 0.01);
  373  	mLayout.setColWidthPerc(2, 0.01);
  374  
  375  	mLayout.setRowHeightPerc(0, 0.02);
  376  
  377  	if (mTitle != nullptr &&  mTitle->getFont() != nullptr)
  378  		mLayout.setRowHeightPerc(1, mTitle->getFont()->getHeight(2.0f) / mSize.y());
  379  
  380  	mLayout.setRowHeightPerc(2, 0.02);
  381  	mLayout.setRowHeightPerc(4, helpSize );
  382  
  383  	mLayout.setSize(mSize);
  384  }
  385  
  386  void GuiSaveState::render(const Transform4x4f& parentTrans)
  387  {
  388  	GuiComponent::render(parentTrans);
  389  
  390  	// The layout keeps its bottom row free for the help prompts
  391  	// (onSizeChanged), but with full-screen menus on -- every handheld
  392  	// panel -- Window draws no help while a second page is open, so on a
  393  	// 640x480 panel this page never showed BACK / LAUNCH / DELETE / COPY TO
  394  	// FREE SLOT at all (2026-09-13, found framing #27). Draw them here, as
  395  	// ViewController does when it is the top page, into the row kept for
  396  	// them; Window then skips its own draw for this frame.
  397  	if (mWindow->peekGui() == this && Renderer::ScreenSettings::fullScreenMenus())
  398  		mWindow->renderHelpPromptsEarly(parentTrans);
  399  }
  400  
  401  void GuiSaveState::centerWindow()
  402  {
  403  	setSize(Renderer::getScreenWidth(), WINDOW_HEIGHT);
  404  	animateTo(
  405  		Vector2f((Renderer::getScreenWidth() - getSize().x()) / 2, Renderer::getScreenHeight()),
  406  		Vector2f((Renderer::getScreenWidth() - getSize().x()) / 2, Renderer::getScreenHeight() - WINDOW_HEIGHT),
  407  		AnimateFlags::OPACITY | AnimateFlags::POSITION);
  408  }
  409  
  410  bool GuiSaveState::input(InputConfig* config, Input input)
  411  {
  412  	if (input.value != 0 && (config->isMappedTo(BUTTON_BACK, input) || config->isMappedTo("l3", input)))
  413  	{
  414  		delete this;
  415  		return true;
  416  	}
  417  
  418  	if (input.value != 0 && config->isMappedTo(BUTTON_OK, input))
  419  	{
  420  		if (mGrid->size())
  421  		{
  422  			const SaveStateItem& item = mGrid->getSelected();
  423  			mRunCallback(item.saveState);
  424  		}
  425  
  426  		delete this;
  427  		return true;
  428  	}
  429  
  430  	if (input.value != 0 && config->isMappedTo("y", input))
  431  	{
  432  		// Every writer of the saves tree is gated by the transfer lock
  433  		// (D-CLOUD-053): a deletion landing while a sync is reading that
  434  		// tree is the race the maintainer
  435  		// named -- exit a game, the backup starts, delete a save under it.
  436  		// Refused, never waited for (nobody waits, #22 R6), in the words the
  437  		// launch gate uses for the same state.
  438  		const std::string deleteBusy = _("YOUR SAVES ARE SYNCING WITH THE CLOUD.\n\nWAIT FOR IT TO FINISH BEFORE DELETING A SAVE STATE - THE NOTIFICATION AT THE TOP SAYS WHEN IT IS DONE.");
  439  		const std::string busy = savesTreeBusy(deleteBusy);
  440  		if (!busy.empty())
  441  		{
  442  			mWindow->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(mWindow, busy));
  443  			return true;
  444  		}
  445  
  446  		if (mGrid->size())
  447  		{
  448  			Window* window = mWindow;
  449  			mWindow->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(mWindow, _("ARE YOU SURE YOU WANT TO DELETE THIS ITEM?"), _("YES"), 
  450  				[this, window, deleteBusy]
  451  				{
  452  					// Asked again at YES: a sync can start while the question
  453  					// is up, and the deletion is queued from here (#307 PL-068).
  454  					const std::string busyNow = savesTreeBusy(deleteBusy);
  455  					if (!busyNow.empty())
  456  					{
  457  						window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, busyNow));
  458  						return;
  459  					}
  460  
  461  					const SaveStateItem& toDelete = mGrid->getSelected();
  462  
  463  					// The grid also holds the START NEW GAME / START NEW AUTO SAVE
  464  					// placeholders, which have no file: nothing to delete.
  465  					if (toDelete.saveState == nullptr || !toDelete.saveState->isSlotValid() || toDelete.saveState->fileName.empty())
  466  						return;
  467  
  468  					// The tile goes now; the disk follows on the worker, in the order
  469  					// D-CLOUD-053 asks for -- the deletion recorded by --retire, then
  470  					// the files unlinked, both inside the script (D-CLOUD-133) -- one
  471  					// deletion at a time (D-UI-073). This
  472  					// used to run both scripts here, on the interface thread, and the
  473  					// dialog hung after YES for as long as they took: a third of a
  474  					// second on x86_64, a second on the RG SP (#205). The worker is
  475  					// handed two strings and nothing else: not this page, which B or
  476  					// LAUNCH may delete before the retire returns, and not the
  477  					// repository's SaveState, which the next refresh() frees. The
  478  					// other slots keep their numbers (D-UI-069), so there is no
  479  					// rescan to run (D-CLOUD-132).
  480  					SaveStateBookkeeper::deleteLater(toDelete.saveState->fileName, toDelete.saveState->getScreenShot());
  481  
  482  					// No refresh(): the file is still on disk for the moment, and
  483  					// loadGrid() hides what is pending. update() compares the disk
  484  					// with the page when the worker reports the deletion done, and
  485  					// rebuilds only if they differ (#207).
  486  					loadGrid();
  487  				}, 
  488  				_("NO"), nullptr));
  489  		}
  490  
  491  		return true;
  492  	}
  493  	
  494  	if (input.value != 0 && config->isMappedTo("x", input))
  495  	{
  496  		// The same gate DELETE carries (D-CLOUD-053): a copy is a writer of
  497  		// the saves tree, and a sync reading that tree must not meet it. It
  498  		// had none until #206.
  499  		const std::string busy = savesTreeBusy(
  500  			_("YOUR SAVES ARE SYNCING WITH THE CLOUD.\n\nWAIT FOR IT TO FINISH BEFORE COPYING A SAVE STATE - THE NOTIFICATION AT THE TOP SAYS WHEN IT IS DONE."));
  501  		if (!busy.empty())
  502  		{
  503  			mWindow->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(mWindow, busy));
  504  			return true;
  505  		}
  506  
  507  		if (mGrid->size())
  508  		{
  509  			const SaveStateItem& toCopy = mGrid->getSelected();
  510  			if (toCopy.saveState == nullptr || !toCopy.saveState->isSlotValid() || toCopy.saveState->fileName.empty())
  511  				return true;
  512  
  513  			int slot = mRepository->getNextFreeSlot(mGame, toCopy.saveState->config);
  514  			if (slot >= 0)
  515  			{
  516  				if (toCopy.saveState->copyToSlot(slot))
  517  				{
  518  					// The copy is a file no capture mode would ever record
  519  					// (#206): exit mode takes only what a launch wrote, the
  520  					// verify passes adopt nothing new. The manager made it, so
  521  					// the manager records it -- on the worker, as the source's
  522  					// version at the new path; the tile is real already.
  523  					FileData* game = mGame->getSourceFileData();
  524  					SaveStateBookkeeper::recordCopy(toCopy.saveState->makeStateFilename(slot),
  525  						toCopy.saveState->fileName, game->getSystem()->getName(), game->getPath());
  526  					mRepository->refresh();
  527  					loadGrid();
  528  				}
  529  			}
  530  		}
  531  
  532  		return true;
  533  	}
  534  	
  535  	return GuiComponent::input(config, input);
  536  }
  537  
  538  void GuiSaveState::update(int deltaTime)
  539  {
  540  	GuiComponent::update(deltaTime);
  541  
  542  	// A job has landed (D-UI-073): a deletion's files are gone, or a copy's
  543  	// record is written. The page already showed the outcome the frame the
  544  	// player pressed -- the tile hidden, the copy's tile added -- so in the
  545  	// normal case the disk now agrees with the page and there is nothing to
  546  	// draw. Rebuilding anyway tore every tile down and replayed the selection
  547  	// animation a second after YES: the flash of #207. So the page is rebuilt
  548  	// only when the disk disagrees with it (D-UI-074) -- a deletion whose
  549  	// unlink failed brings its tile back, which is the one sign the player
  550  	// gets -- and then keeps the cursor where it is, because a page that
  551  	// jumps to its first tile a second after a press is a page that looks
  552  	// broken. The repository keeps the SaveState of a gone file until its
  553  	// next refresh() (a copy, the next open of the page); onDisk() hands it
  554  	// to nobody in the meantime.
  555  	const unsigned done = SaveStateBookkeeper::completed();
  556  	if (done != mDeletionsSeen)
  557  	{
  558  		mDeletionsSeen = done;
  559  		const std::vector<std::string> files = filesOnDisk();
  560  		if (files != mShown)
  561  		{
  562  			// The exception path, so it is logged: a deletion that did not
  563  			// take, or a file that arrived while the page was open -- seen
  564  			// here, when a job lands, since the disk is read at no other
  565  			// moment (audit #258, the seat's G-07).
  566  			LOG(LogInfo) << "save state manager: a landed job left the disk differing from the page ("
  567  				<< mShown.size() << " shown, " << files.size() << " on disk); rebuilt";
  568  			const int cursor = mGrid->getCursorIndex();
  569  			mRepository->refresh();
  570  			loadGrid();
  571  			if (cursor > 0 && cursor < mGrid->size())
  572  				mGrid->setCursorIndex(cursor);
  573  		}
  574  	}
  575  }
  576  
  577  std::vector<HelpPrompt> GuiSaveState::getHelpPrompts()
  578  {
  579  	std::vector<HelpPrompt> prompts;
  580  	prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt(BUTTON_BACK, _("BACK"), [&] { delete this; }));
  581  	prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt(BUTTON_OK, _("LAUNCH")));
  582  
  583  	if (mGrid->size())
  584  	{
  585  		const SaveStateItem& item = mGrid->getSelected();
  586  		if (!item.saveState->fileName.empty())
  587  		{
  588  			prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt("y", _("DELETE")));
  589  			prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt("x", _("COPY TO FREE SLOT")));
  590  		}
  591  	}	
  592  
  593  	return prompts;
  594  }
  595  
  596  bool GuiSaveState::hitTest(int x, int y, Transform4x4f& parentTransform, std::vector<GuiComponent*>* pResult)
  597  {
  598  	if (pResult) pResult->push_back(this); // Always return this as it's a fake fullscreen, so we always have click events
  599  	GuiComponent::hitTest(x, y, parentTransform, pResult);
  600  	return true;
  601  }
  602  
  603  bool GuiSaveState::onMouseClick(int button, bool pressed, int x, int y)
  604  {
  605  	if (pressed && button == 1 && !mBackground.isMouseOver())
  606  	{
  607  		delete this;
  608  		return true;
  609  	}
  610  
  611  	return (button == 1);
  612  }
```

   58  void GuiScraperStart::loadScrapPage() 
   59  {
   60  	mOverwriteMedias = true;
   61  
   62  	std::string scraperName = Settings::getInstance()->getString("Scraper");
   63  
   64  	// scrape from
   65  	auto scraper_list = std::make_shared< OptionListComponent< std::string > >(mWindow, _("SCRAPING DATABASE"), false);
   66  
   67  	// Select either the first entry of the one read from the settings, just in case the scraper from settings has vanished.
   68  	for (auto engine : Scraper::getScraperList())
   69  		scraper_list->add(engine, engine, engine == scraperName);
   70  
   71  
   72  	addGroup(_("SOURCE"));
   73  	addWithLabel(_("SCRAPE FROM"), scraper_list);
   74  
   75  	if (!scraper_list->hasSelection())
   76  	{
   77  		scraper_list->selectFirstItem();
   78  		scraperName = scraper_list->getSelected();
   79  	}	
   80  
   81  	addGroup(_("FILTERS"));
   82  
   83  	auto scraper = Scraper::getScraper(scraperName);
   84  
   85  	// GAMES TO SCRAPE FOR, IGNORE RECENTLY SCRAPED GAMES and SYSTEMS INCLUDED are
   86  	// remembered (#67). They used to be rebuilt with hard-coded defaults on every
   87  	// visit and on every tab switch, so a choice made before stepping to OPTIONS
   88  	// was gone on the way back. Each is written by a save function, which runs
   89  	// when the tab changes and when the page closes -- the path the OPTIONS rows
   90  	// already use.
   91  
   92  	// Media Filter
   93  	std::string mediaFilter = Settings::getInstance()->getString("ScraperFilter");
   94  	if (mediaFilter != "all" && mediaFilter != "missing-all")
   95  		mediaFilter = "missing-any";
   96  
   97  	mFilters = std::make_shared< OptionListComponent<FilterFunc> >(mWindow, _("GAMES TO SCRAPE FOR"), false);
   98  	mFilters->add(_("ALL"), [](FileData*) -> bool { return true; }, mediaFilter == "all");
   99  	mFilters->add(_("GAMES MISSING ANY MEDIA"), [this, scraper](FileData* g) -> bool { mOverwriteMedias = false; return scraper->hasMissingMedia(g); }, mediaFilter == "missing-any");
  100  	mFilters->add(_("GAMES MISSING ALL MEDIA"), [this, scraper](FileData* g) -> bool { mOverwriteMedias = true; return !scraper->hasAnyMedia(g); }, mediaFilter == "missing-all");
  101  	addWithLabel(_("GAMES TO SCRAPE FOR"), mFilters);
  102  	addSaveFunc([this]
  103  	{
  104  		static const char* keys[] = { "all", "missing-any", "missing-all" };
  105  		int i = mFilters->getSelectedIndex();
  106  		if (i >= 0 && i < 3)
  107  			Settings::getInstance()->setString("ScraperFilter", keys[i]);
  108  	});
  109  
  110  	// Date Filter
  111  	auto now = Utils::Time::now();
  112  	auto isOlderThan = [now](const std::string& scraper, FileData* f, int days)
  113  	{
  114  		auto date = f->getMetadata().getScrapeDate(scraper);
  115  		if (date == nullptr || !date->isValid())
  116  			return true;
  117  
  118  		return date->getTime() <= (now - (days * 86400));
  119  	};
  120  
  121  	// Upstream declared this setting (default 3, LAST 15 DAYS) and never read it.
  122  	int idx = Settings::getInstance()->getInt("RecentlyScrappedFilter");
  123  	if (idx < 0 || idx > 6)
  124  		idx = 3;
  125  
  126  	mDateFilters = std::make_shared< OptionListComponent<FilterFunc> >(mWindow, _("IGNORE RECENTLY SCRAPED GAMES"), false);
  127  	mDateFilters->add(_("NO"), [](FileData*) -> bool { return true; }, idx == 0);
  128  	mDateFilters->add(_("LAST DAY"), [this, scraperName, isOlderThan](FileData* g) -> bool { return isOlderThan(scraperName, g, 1); }, idx == 1);
  129  	mDateFilters->add(_("LAST WEEK"), [this, scraperName, isOlderThan](FileData* g) -> bool { return isOlderThan(scraperName, g, 7); }, idx == 2);
  130  	mDateFilters->add(_("LAST 15 DAYS"), [this, scraperName, isOlderThan](FileData* g) -> bool { return isOlderThan(scraperName, g, 15); }, idx == 3);
  131  	mDateFilters->add(_("LAST MONTH"), [this, scraperName, isOlderThan](FileData* g) -> bool { return isOlderThan(scraperName, g, 31); }, idx == 4);
  132  	mDateFilters->add(_("LAST 3 MONTHS"), [this, scraperName, isOlderThan](FileData* g) -> bool { return isOlderThan(scraperName, g, 90); }, idx == 5);
  133  	mDateFilters->add(_("LAST YEAR"), [this, scraperName, isOlderThan](FileData* g) -> bool { return isOlderThan(scraperName, g, 365); }, idx == 6);
  134  	addWithLabel(_("IGNORE RECENTLY SCRAPED GAMES"), mDateFilters);
  135  	addSaveFunc([this]
  136  	{
  137  		int i = mDateFilters->getSelectedIndex();
  138  		if (i >= 0)
  139  			Settings::getInstance()->setInt("RecentlyScrappedFilter", i);
  140  	});
  141  
  142  	// System Filter
  143  	std::string currentSystem;
  144  
  145  	if (ViewController::get()->getState().viewing == ViewController::GAME_LIST)
  146  	{
  147  		auto uiSystem = ViewController::get()->getState().getSystem();
  148  		if (uiSystem != nullptr)
  149  		{
  150  			if (uiSystem->isGroupSystem())
  151  			{
  152  				auto gl = ViewController::get()->getGameListView(uiSystem, false);
  153  				if (gl != nullptr && gl->getCursor() != nullptr)
  154  					uiSystem = gl->getCursor()->getSourceFileData()->getSystem();
  155  			}
  156  
  157  			if (uiSystem != nullptr)
  158  				currentSystem = uiSystem->getName();
  159  		}
  160  	}
  161  
  162  	// Opened from a game list, the page pre-selects that system ("scrape this
  163  	// one"). Opened from the main menu, it restores the set used last time, and
  164  	// falls back to today's default -- every system with a platform id -- when
  165  	// nothing was remembered or the remembered names match no listed system.
  166  	std::set<std::string> remembered;
  167  	for (auto& name : Utils::String::split(Settings::getInstance()->getString("ScraperSystems"), ','))
  168  		if (!name.empty())
  169  			remembered.insert(name);
  170  
  171  	std::vector<SystemData*> listed;
  172  	bool anyRemembered = false;
  173  	for (auto system : SystemData::sSystemVector)
  174  		if (system->isGameSystem() && !system->isCollection() && !system->hasPlatformId(PlatformIds::PLATFORM_IGNORE) && scraper->isSupportedPlatform(system))
  175  		{
  176  			listed.push_back(system);
  177  			if (remembered.count(system->getName()))
  178  				anyRemembered = true;
  179  		}
  180  
  181  	bool useRemembered = currentSystem.empty() && anyRemembered;
  182  
  183  	mSystems = std::make_shared<OptionListComponent<SystemData*>>(mWindow, _("SYSTEMS INCLUDED"), true);
  184  
  185  	for (auto system : listed)
  186  	{
  187  		bool selected;
  188  		if (!currentSystem.empty())
  189  			selected = system->getName() == currentSystem && !system->getPlatformIds().empty();
  190  		else if (useRemembered)
  191  			selected = remembered.count(system->getName()) > 0;
  192  		else
  193  			selected = !system->getPlatformIds().empty();
  194  
  195  		mSystems->add(system->getFullName(), system, selected);
  196  	}
  197  
  198  	addWithLabel(_("SYSTEMS INCLUDED"), mSystems);
  199  
  200  	// A game list's pre-selection is contextual; only a set chosen from the main
  201  	// menu is worth remembering.
  202  	if (currentSystem.empty())
  203  		addSaveFunc([this]
  204  		{
  205  			std::string names;
  206  			for (auto system : mSystems->getSelectedObjects())
  207  				names += (names.empty() ? "" : ",") + system->getName();
  208  
  209  			Settings::getInstance()->setString("ScraperSystems", names);
  210  		});
  211  
  212  	mMenu.clearButtons();
  213  	mMenu.addButton(_("SCRAPE NOW"), _("START"), std::bind(&GuiScraperStart::pressedStart, this));
  214  
  215  	scraper_list->setSelectedChangedCallback([this, scraperName, scraper_list](std::string value) { Settings::getInstance()->setString("Scraper", value); });
  216  }

  428  void GuiScraperStart::pressedStart()
  429  {
  430  #if defined(SCREENSCRAPER_RUNTIME_DEV_LOGIN) && !defined(SCREENSCRAPER_DEV_LOGIN)
  431  	// Without a developer pair the API refuses every request with a French
  432  	// login error. Say what is missing and where it goes: the pair's rows
  433  	// are on the ACCOUNTS tab, under the account they belong to
  434  	// (loadAccountsPage), which this and the rejected-pair message both
  435  	// used to place under OPTIONS (#151 PL-15).
  436  	if (Settings::getInstance()->getString("Scraper") == "ScreenScraper" && screenScraperDevLogin().empty())
  437  	{
  438  		mWindow->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(mWindow,
  439  			_("SCREENSCRAPER NEEDS A DEVELOPER ID AND PASSWORD.\nENTER YOURS UNDER SCRAPER > ACCOUNTS.")));
  440  		return;
  441  	}
  442  #endif
  443  
  444  	std::vector<SystemData*> systems = mSystems->getSelectedObjects();
  445  	for(auto system : systems)
  446  	{
  447  		if (system->getPlatformIds().empty())
  448  		{
  449  			mWindow->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(mWindow, 
  450  				_("WARNING: SOME OF YOUR SELECTED SYSTEMS DO NOT HAVE A PLATFORM SET. RESULTS MAY BE FOR DIFFERENT SYSTEMS!\nCONTINUE ANYWAY?"),  
  451  				_("YES"), std::bind(&GuiScraperStart::start, this),  
  452  				_("NO"), nullptr)); 
  453  
  454  			return;
  455  		}
  456  	}
  457  
  458  	start();
  459  }
  460  
  461  void GuiScraperStart::start()
  462  {
  463  	if (ThreadedScraper::isRunning())
  464  	{
  465  		Window* window = mWindow;
  466  
  467  		mWindow->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(mWindow,
  468  			_("SCRAPING IS RUNNING. DO YOU WANT TO STOP IT?"),
  469  			_("YES"), [this, window] { ThreadedScraper::stop(); },
  470  			_("NO"), nullptr));
  471  
  472  		return;
  473  	}
  474  
  475  	mWindow->pushGui(new GuiLoading<std::queue<ScraperSearchParams>>(mWindow, _("PLEASE WAIT"),
  476  		[this](IGuiLoadingHandler* gui)
  477  		{
  478  			return getSearches(mSystems->getSelectedObjects(), mFilters->getSelected(), mDateFilters->getSelected(), gui);
  479  		},
  480  		[this](std::queue<ScraperSearchParams> searches)
  481  		{
  482  			if (searches.empty())
  483  				mWindow->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(mWindow, _("NO GAMES FIT THAT CRITERIA.")));
  484  			else
  485  			{
  486  				// The run's page over this one, then this one goes: the
  487  				// scrape is watched to its end (D-UI-078), and the outcome
  488  				// is dismissed back onto the game lists.
  489  				ThreadedScraper::start(mWindow, searches);
  490  				if (ThreadedScraper::isRunning())
  491  					mWindow->pushGui(new GuiScraperRun(mWindow));
  492  				close();
  493  			}
  494  		}));
  495  }
  496  
  497  std::queue<ScraperSearchParams> GuiScraperStart::getSearches(std::vector<SystemData*> systems, FilterFunc mediaSelector, FilterFunc dateSelector, IGuiLoadingHandler* handler)
  498  {
  499  	std::queue<ScraperSearchParams> queue;
  500  
  501  	int pos = 1;
  502  	for (auto system : systems)
  503  	{		
  504  		if (systems.size() > 0 && handler != nullptr)
  505  		{
  506  			int percent = (pos * 100) / systems.size();
  507  			handler->setText(_("PLEASE WAIT") + " " + std::to_string(percent) + "%");
  508  		}
  509  
  510  		auto games = system->getRootFolder()->getFilesRecursive(GAME);
  511  		for(auto game : games)
  512  		{
  513  			if (dateSelector(game) && mediaSelector(game))
  514  			{
  515  				ScraperSearchParams search;
  516  				search.game = game;
  517  				search.system = system;
  518  				search.overWriteMedias = mOverwriteMedias;
  519  
  520  				queue.push(search);
  521  			}
  522  		}
  523  
  524  		pos++;
  525  	}
  526  
  527  	return queue;
  528  }
  529  
  530  bool GuiScraperStart::input(InputConfig* config, Input input)
  531  {
  532  	bool consumed = GuiSettings::input(config, input);
  533  	if (consumed)
  534  		return true;
  535  
  536  	if (input.value != 0 && config->isMappedTo(BUTTON_BACK, input))
  537  	{
  538  		close();
  539  		return true;
  540  	}
  541  
  542  	if (config->isMappedTo("start", input) && input.value != 0)
  543  	{
  544  		// close everything
  545  		Window* window = mWindow;
  546  		while (window->peekGui() && window->peekGui() != ViewController::get())
  547  			delete window->peekGui();
  548  	}
  549  
  550  	return false;
  551  }
  552  
  553  std::vector<HelpPrompt> GuiScraperStart::getHelpPrompts()
  554  {
  555  	std::vector<HelpPrompt> prompts;
  556  	prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt(BUTTON_BACK, _("BACK")));
  557  	prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt("start", _("CLOSE")));
  558  	return prompts;
  559  }

   83  void GuiSettings::save()
   84  {

   87  
   88  	if(!mSaveFuncs.size())
   89  		return;
   90

   92  		(*it)();
