  173  void GuiSettings::buildInputTextConfigRow(const std::string& title, const std::string& settingsID, bool password, bool storeInSettings
  174  	, const std::function<void(Window*, std::string/*title*/, std::string /*value*/, const std::function<void(std::string)>& onsave)>& customEditor)
  175  {
  176  	auto theme = ThemeData::getMenuTheme();
  177  	std::shared_ptr<Font> font = theme->Text.font;
  178  	unsigned int color = theme->Text.color;
  179  
  180  	// LABEL
  181  	Window *window = mWindow;
  182  	ComponentListRow row;
  183  
  184  	auto lbl = std::make_shared<TextComponent>(window, title, font, color);
  185  	if (EsLocale::isRTL())
  186  		lbl->setHorizontalAlignment(Alignment::ALIGN_RIGHT);
  187  
  188  	row.addElement(lbl, true); // label
  189  
  190  	std::string value = storeInSettings ? Settings::getInstance()->getString(settingsID) : SystemConf::getInstance()->get(settingsID);
  191  
  192  	std::string text = ((password && value != "") ? "*********" : value);
  193  	std::shared_ptr<TextComponent> ed = std::make_shared<TextComponent>(window, text, font, color, ALIGN_RIGHT);
  194  	if (EsLocale::isRTL())
  195  		ed->setHorizontalAlignment(Alignment::ALIGN_LEFT);
  196  		
  197  	// ed->setRenderBackground(true); ed->setBackgroundColor(0xFFFF00FF); // Debug only
  198  	
  199  	ed->setSize(font->sizeText(text+"  ").x(), 0);
  200  	row.addElement(ed, false);
  201  
  202  	auto spacer = std::make_shared<GuiComponent>(mWindow);
  203  	spacer->setSize(Renderer::getScreenWidth() * 0.005f, 0);
  204  	row.addElement(spacer, false);
  205  
  206  	auto bracket = std::make_shared<ImageComponent>(mWindow);
  207  	bracket->setImage(theme->Icons.arrow);
  208  	bracket->setResize(Vector2f(0, font->getLetterHeight()));
  209  
  210  	if (EsLocale::isRTL())
  211  		bracket->setFlipX(true);
  212  
  213  	row.addElement(bracket, false);
  214  
  215  	// it needs a local copy for the lambdas
  216  	std::string localSettingsID = settingsID;
  217  
  218  	auto updateVal = [this, font, ed, localSettingsID, password, storeInSettings](const std::string &newVal)
  219  	{
  220  		if (!password)
  221  		{
  222  			ed->setValue(newVal);
  223  			ed->setSize(font->sizeText(newVal + "  ").x(), 0);
  224  
  225  			mMenu.updateSize();
  226  		}
  227  		else
  228  		{
  229  			ed->setValue("*********");
  230  			ed->setSize(font->sizeText("*********  ").x(), 0);
  231  			mMenu.updateSize();
  232  		}
  233  
  234  		if (storeInSettings)
  235  			Settings::getInstance()->setString(localSettingsID, newVal);
  236  		else
  237  			SystemConf::getInstance()->set(localSettingsID, newVal);
  238  	}; // ok callback (apply new value to ed)
  239  
  240  	bool localStoreInSettings = storeInSettings;
  241  	row.makeAcceptInputHandler([this, title, updateVal, localSettingsID, localStoreInSettings, customEditor, password]
  242  	{
  243  		std::string data = localStoreInSettings ? Settings::getInstance()->getString(localSettingsID) : SystemConf::getInstance()->get(localSettingsID);
  244  
  245  #if defined(BATOCERA) || defined(ROCKNIX)
  246  		if (password && (SystemConf::getInstance()->get("system.security.enabled") == "1"))
  247  			data.clear();
  248  #endif
  249  		if (customEditor != nullptr)
  250  			customEditor(mWindow, title, data, updateVal);
  251  		else if (Settings::getInstance()->getBool("UseOSK"))
  252  			mWindow->pushGui(new GuiTextEditPopupKeyboard(mWindow, title, data, updateVal, false));
  253  		else
  254  			mWindow->pushGui(new GuiTextEditPopup(mWindow, title, data, updateVal, false));
  255  	});
  256  
  257  	addRow(row);
  258  }

  262  	, const std::function<void(std::string)>& onsave)

  302  	{
  303  		if (!password)
  304  		{
  305  			ed->setValue(newVal);
  306  			ed->setSize(font->sizeText(newVal + "  ").x(), 0);
  307  
  308  			mMenu.updateSize();
  309  		}
  310  		else
  311  		{
  312  			ed->setValue("*********");
  313  			ed->setSize(font->sizeText("*********  ").x(), 0);
  314  			mMenu.updateSize();
  315  		}
  316  
  317  		onsave(newVal);
  318  
  319  	}; // ok callback (apply new value to ed)
  320  
  321  	row.makeAcceptInputHandler([this, title, updateVal, customEditor, value, password]
  322  	{
  323  	  std::string data = value;
  324  #ifdef BATOCERA
  325  		if (password && (SystemConf::getInstance()->get("system.security.enabled") == "1"))
  326  			data.clear();
  327  #endif
  328  		if (customEditor != nullptr)
  329  			customEditor(mWindow, title, data, updateVal);
  330  		else if (Settings::getInstance()->getBool("UseOSK"))
  331  			mWindow->pushGui(new GuiTextEditPopupKeyboard(mWindow, title, data, updateVal, false));
  332  		else
  333  			mWindow->pushGui(new GuiTextEditPopup(mWindow, title, data, updateVal, false));
  334  	});
  335  
  336  	addRow(row);
  337  }
  338  
  339  void GuiSettings::addFileBrowser(const std::string& title, const std::string& settingsID, GuiFileBrowser::FileTypes type, bool storeInSettings)
  340  {
  341  	Window* window = mWindow;
  342  
  343  	std::string value = storeInSettings ? Settings::getInstance()->getString(settingsID) : SystemConf::getInstance()->get(settingsID);
  344  
  345  	auto theme = ThemeData::getMenuTheme();
  346  
  347  	ComponentListRow row;
  348  
  349  	auto lbl = std::make_shared<TextComponent>(window, Utils::String::toUpper(title), theme->Text.font, theme->Text.color);
  350  	lbl->setSize(theme->Text.font->sizeText(Utils::String::toUpper(title) + "  ").x(), 0);
  351  	row.addElement(lbl, false); // label
  352  
  353  	std::shared_ptr<TextComponent> ed = std::make_shared<TextComponent>(window, "", theme->Text.font, theme->Text.color, ALIGN_RIGHT);
  354  	row.addElement(ed, true);
  355  
  356  	auto spacer = std::make_shared<GuiComponent>(window);
  357  	spacer->setSize(Renderer::getScreenWidth() * 0.005f, 0);
  358  	row.addElement(spacer, false);
  359  
  360  	auto bracket = std::make_shared<ImageComponent>(window);
  361  	bracket->setImage(ThemeData::getMenuTheme()->Icons.arrow);
  362  	bracket->setResize(Vector2f(0, lbl->getFont()->getLetterHeight()));
  363  	row.addElement(bracket, false);
  364  
  365  	std::string localSettingsID = settingsID;
  366  	bool localStoreInSettings = storeInSettings;
  367  
  368  	auto updateVal = [this, ed, localSettingsID, localStoreInSettings](const std::string &newVal)
  369  	{
  370  		ed->setValue(newVal);			
  371  		mMenu.updateSize();
  372  
  373  		if (localStoreInSettings)
  374  			Settings::getInstance()->setString(localSettingsID, newVal);
  375  		else
  376  			SystemConf::getInstance()->set(localSettingsID, newVal);
  377  	};
  378  
  379  	row.makeAcceptInputHandler([window, title, type, ed, updateVal]
  380  	{
  381  		auto parent = Utils::FileSystem::getParent(ed->getValue());
  382  		window->pushGui(new GuiFileBrowser(window, parent, ed->getValue(), type, updateVal, title));
  383  	});
  384  
  385  	ed->setValue(value);
  386  
  387  
  388  	addRow(row);
  389  }
  390  
  391  std::shared_ptr<SwitchComponent> GuiSettings::addSwitch(const std::string& title, const std::string& description, const std::string& settingsID, bool storeInSettings, const std::function<void()>& onChanged)
  392  {
  393  	Window* window = mWindow;
  394  
  395  	bool value = storeInSettings ? Settings::getInstance()->getBool(settingsID) : SystemConf::getInstance()->getBool(settingsID);
  396  
  397  	auto comp = std::make_shared<SwitchComponent>(mWindow);
  398  	comp->setState(value);
  399  
  400  	if (!description.empty())
  401  		addWithDescription(title, description, comp);
  402  	else 
  403  		addWithLabel(title, comp);
  404  
  405  	std::string localSettingsID = settingsID;
  406  	bool localStoreInSettings = storeInSettings;
  407  
  408  	addSaveFunc([comp, localStoreInSettings, localSettingsID, onChanged]
  409  	{
  410  		bool changed = localStoreInSettings ? Settings::getInstance()->setBool(localSettingsID, comp->getState()) : SystemConf::getInstance()->setBool(localSettingsID, comp->getState());
  411  		if (changed && onChanged != nullptr)
  412  			onChanged();
  413  	});
  414  
  415  	return comp;
  416  }
  417  
  418  std::shared_ptr<OptionListComponent<std::string>> GuiSettings::addOptionList(const std::string& title, const std::string& description, const std::vector<std::pair<std::string, std::string>>& values, const std::string& settingsID, bool storeInSettings, const std::function<void()>& onChanged)
  419  {
  420  	Window* window = mWindow;
  421  
  422  	std::string value = storeInSettings ? Settings::getInstance()->getString(settingsID) : SystemConf::getInstance()->get(settingsID);
  423  
  424  	auto comp = std::make_shared<OptionListComponent<std::string>>(mWindow, title, false);
  425  	comp->addRange(values, value);
  426  
  427  	if (!description.empty())
  428  		addWithDescription(title, description, comp);
  429  	else 
  430  		addWithLabel(title, comp);
  431  
  432  	std::string localSettingsID = settingsID;
  433  	bool localStoreInSettings = storeInSettings;
  434  
  435  	addSaveFunc([comp, localStoreInSettings, localSettingsID, onChanged]
  436  	{
  437  		bool changed = localStoreInSettings ? Settings::getInstance()->setString(localSettingsID, comp->getSelected()) : SystemConf::getInstance()->set(localSettingsID, comp->getSelected());
  438  		if (changed && onChanged != nullptr)
  439  			onChanged();
  440  	});
  441  
  442  	return comp;
  443  }
  444  
  445  bool GuiSettings::checkNetwork()
  446  {
  447  	if (ApiSystem::getInstance()->getIpAddress() == "NOT CONNECTED")
  448  	{
  449  		mWindow->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(mWindow, _("YOU ARE NOT CONNECTED TO A NETWORK"), _("OK"), nullptr));
  450  		return false;
  451  	}
  452  
  453  	return true;
  454  }
  455  
  456  bool GuiSettings::onMouseClick(int button, bool pressed, int x, int y)
  457  {
  458  	if (pressed && button == 1 && !mMenu.isMouseOver())
  459  	{
  460  		delete this;
  461  		return true;
  462  	}
  463  
  464  	return (button == 1);
  465  }

  104  	// A token that dies with the page. A worker that reports back through
  105  	// Window::postToUiThread keeps a std::weak_ptr of it and does nothing
  106  	// when lock() fails: the page it was going to fill in has been closed,
  107  	// and there is nothing left to write to. Held here rather than in a
  108  	// save function so that adding one does not turn a page with nothing
  109  	// to save into one that writes the settings files on close.
  110  	std::shared_ptr<void> lifeToken()

    5  #ifndef EMULATIONSTATION_ALL_GUILOADING_H
    6  #define EMULATIONSTATION_ALL_GUILOADING_H
    7  
    8  #include "Window.h"
    9  #include "GuiComponent.h"
   10  #include "components/MenuComponent.h"
   11  #include "components/BusyComponent.h"

   13  #include <string>
   14  #include <thread>
   15  #include <mutex>
   16  #include "Settings.h"
   17  #include "animations/LambdaAnimation.h"
   18  
   19  class IGuiLoadingHandler
   20  {
   21  public:
   22  	virtual void setText(const std::string& text) = 0;
   23  };
   24  
   25  template<typename T>
   26  class GuiLoading : public GuiComponent, public IGuiLoadingHandler
   27  {
   28  public:
   29  	GuiLoading(Window *window, const std::string title, const std::function<T(IGuiLoadingHandler*)> &func, const std::function<void(T)> &func2 = nullptr)
   30  		: GuiComponent(window), mBusyAnim(window), mFunc(func), mFunc2(func2)
   31  	{
   32  		setSize((float)Renderer::getScreenWidth(), (float)Renderer::getScreenHeight());
   33  		setTag("GuiLoading");
   34  	
   35  		mText = title;
   36  		mTextChanged = false;
   37  
   38  		mRunning = true;
   39  		mHandle = new std::thread(&GuiLoading::threadLoading, this);
   40  		mBusyAnim.setText(title);
   41  		mBusyAnim.setSize(mSize);
   42  
   43  		mBusyAnim.setOpacity(0);
   44  		auto fadeFunc = [this](float t) { mBusyAnim.setOpacity((unsigned char) (Math::easeOutCubic(t) * 255.0f)); };
   45  		setAnimation(new LambdaAnimation(fadeFunc, 250), 0, [this] { mBusyAnim.setOpacity(255); });
   46  	}
   47  	
   48  	~GuiLoading()
   49  	{
   50  		mRunning = false;
   51  		mHandle->join();
   52  	}
   53  
   54  	void setText(const std::string& text) override
   55  	{		
   56  		mTextMutex.lock();
   57  
   58  		if (mText != text)
   59  		{
   60  			mText = text;
   61  			mTextChanged = true;
   62  		}
   63  
   64  		mTextMutex.unlock();
   65  	}
   66  
   67  	void render(const Transform4x4f &parentTrans) override
   68  	{
   69  		if (!mRunning || !mVisible)
   70  			return;
   71  	
   72  		Transform4x4f trans = parentTrans * getTransform();
   73  		renderChildren(trans);
   74  	
   75  		Renderer::setMatrix(trans);
   76  		mBusyAnim.render(trans);
   77  	}
   78  
   79  	bool input(InputConfig *config, Input input) override
   80  	{
   81  		return false;	
   82  	}
   83  	
   84  	void update(int deltaTime) override
   85  	{
   86  		GuiComponent::update(deltaTime);
   87  
   88  		updateText();
   89  
   90  		mBusyAnim.update(deltaTime);
   91  
   92  		if (!mRunning)
   93  		{
   94  			if (mFunc2)
   95  			{
   96  				Window* window = mWindow;
   97  				auto func = mFunc2;
   98  				auto ret = result;
   99  
  100  				delete this;
  101  
  102  				func(ret);
  103  			}
  104  			else
  105  				delete this;
  106  		}
  107  	}
  108  	
  109  private:
  110  	void threadLoading()
  111  	{
  112  		result = mFunc(this);
  113  		mRunning = false;
  114  	}
  115  
  116  	void updateText()
  117  	{
  118  		mTextMutex.lock();
  119  
  120  		if (mTextChanged)
  121  		{
  122  			mBusyAnim.setText(mText);
  123  			mBusyAnim.setSize(mSize);
  124  			mTextChanged = false;
  125  		}
  126  
  127  		mTextMutex.unlock();
  128  	}
  129  
  130      BusyComponent	mBusyAnim;
  131      std::thread*	mHandle;
  132      bool			mRunning;
  133  
  134  	std::mutex		mTextMutex;
  135  	std::string		mText;
  136  	bool			mTextChanged;
  137  
  138      const std::function<T(IGuiLoadingHandler*)> mFunc;
  139      const std::function<void(T)> mFunc2;
  140      T result;
  141  };
  142  
  143  #endif //EMULATIONSTATION_ALL_GUILOADING_H

   19  class MoonlightClient {
   20   public:
   21    MoonlightClient(const std::string& server_ip)
   22     : server_ip_(server_ip) {}
   23    
   24    // Requests to Moonlight server
   25    std::string GetAppListXml() { return MakeRequest(MakeUrl("applist")); }
   26    void QuitApp() { MakeRequest(MakeUrl("cancel")); }
   27    bool WriteBoxArtFile(const std::string& app_id, std::string filename);

   31  
   32   private:
   33    std::string MakeUrl(const std::string& command, std::string args={});

   36  
   37    std::string server_ip_;
   38  };
   39  
   40  // Moonlight version check function
   41  bool isEmbedded(void) {
   42    FILE* mlver = popen ("moonlight -v", "r");
   43    std::stringstream ss;
   44    ss << mlver;
   45    std::string output = ss.str(); 
   46    if (output.find("Embedded") != std::string::npos) {
   47      return true;
   48    } else {
   49      return false;
   50    }
   51  }
   52  
   53  // SSL extraction function (client.pem)
   54  void extractClient() {
   55    std::ifstream confFile("/storage/.config/Moonlight Game Streaming Project/Moonlight.conf");
   56    std::ofstream clientFile("/storage/.cache/Moonlight Game Streaming Project/client.pem");
   57    std::string line;
   58    do{
   59      std::getline(confFile, line);
   60    } 
   61    while (line.find("certificate=") == std::string::npos);
   62    if (line.find("certificate=\"") != std::string::npos) {
   63      line.erase(0, 24);
   64      line.erase(line.size()-2);
   65    } else {
   66      line.erase(0, 23);
   67      line.erase(line.size()-1);
   68    }
   69    std::string::size_type pos = 0;
   70    do{
   71      pos = line.find("\\n", pos);
   72      line.replace(pos, 2, "\n");
   73    }
   74    while (line.find("\\n", pos) != std::string::npos);
   75    clientFile << line;
   76  }
   77  
   78  // SSL extraction function (key.pem)
   79  void extractKey() {
   80    std::ifstream confFile("/storage/.config/Moonlight Game Streaming Project/Moonlight.conf");
   81    std::ofstream keyFile("/storage/.cache/Moonlight Game Streaming Project/key.pem");
   82    std::string line;
   83    do{
   84      std::getline(confFile, line);
   85    }
   86    while (line.find("key=*** == std::string::npos);
   87    if (line.find("key=*** != std::string::npos) {
   88      line.erase(0, 16);
   89      line.erase(line.size()-2);
   90    } else {
   91      line.erase(0, 15);
   92      line.erase(line.size()-1);
   93    }
   94    std::string::size_type pos = 0;
   95    do{
   96      pos = line.find("\\n", pos);
   97      line.replace(pos, 2, "\n");
   98    }
   99    while (line.find("\\n", pos) != std::string::npos);
  100    keyFile << line;
  101  }
  102  
  103  // File existence check function
  104  bool fileExists (const std::string& name) {
  105    if (FILE *file = fopen(name.c_str(), "r")) {
  106        fclose(file);
  107        return true;
  108    } else {
  109        return false;
  110    }   
  111  }
  112  
  113  std::string MoonlightClient::CreateNewGuid() {
  114    srand(time(NULL));
  115  
  116    char strUuid[256];
  117    snprintf(strUuid, sizeof strUuid, "%x%x-%x-%x-%x-%x%x%x", 
  118        rand(), rand(),                 // Generates a 64-bit Hex number
  119        rand(),                         // Generates a 32-bit Hex number
  120        ((rand() & 0x0fff) | 0x4000),   // Generates a 32-bit Hex number of the form 4xxx (4 indicates the UUID version)
  121        rand() % 0x3fff + 0x8000,       // Generates a 32-bit Hex number in the range [0x8000, 0xbfff]
  122        rand(), rand(), rand());
  123    return strUuid;
  124  }
  125  
  126  std::string MoonlightClient::MakeUrl(
  127      const std::string& command, std::string args) {
  128    if (!args.empty()) args += "&";
  129    args += "uniqueid=0123456789ABCDEF&uuid=" + CreateNewGuid();
  130  
  131    char url[1024];
  132    snprintf(url, sizeof url, "https://%s:47984/%s?%s",
  133        server_ip_.c_str(), command.c_str(), args.c_str());
  134    return std::string(url);
  135  }
  136  
  137  std::string MoonlightClient::MakeRequest(const std::string& url, std::string* filename) {
  138    std::string cert_path;
  139    if (fileExists("/storage/.config/Moonlight Game Streaming Project/Moonlight.conf") == true) {
  140      cert_path = "/storage/.cache/Moonlight Game Streaming Project/";
  141    } else {
  142      cert_path = "/storage/.cache/moonlight/";
  143    }
  144  
  145    HttpReqOptions opts;
  146    opts.clientCert = cert_path + "client.pem";
  147    opts.clientKey = cert_path + "key.pem";
  148  
  149    if (filename != nullptr)
  150      opts.outputFilename = *filename;
  151  
  152  	HttpReq req(url, &opts);
  153    std::cout << "MoonlightClient::MakeRequest : " << url << std::endl;
  154  	req.wait();
  155  	
  156  	if (req.status() != HttpReq::REQ_SUCCESS) return "";
  157    if (filename) {
  158      std::cout << "MoonlightClient::MakeRequest saved to : " << *filename << std::endl;
  159      return {};
  160    } else {
  161      auto res = req.getContent();
  162      std::cout << "MoonlightClient::MakeRequest got : " << res << std::endl;
  163      return res;
  164    }
  165  }
  166  
  167  bool MoonlightClient::WriteBoxArtFile(const std::string& app_id, std::string filename) {
  168    char args[256];
  169    snprintf(args, sizeof args, "appid=%s&AssetType=2&AssetIdx=0", app_id.c_str());
  170    MakeRequest(MakeUrl("appasset", args), &filename);
  171    return true;
  172  }
  173  
  174  bool MoonlightClient::UpdateMoonlightGames() {
  175    ApiSystem::executeScriptLegacy("rm /storage/roms/moonlight/images/*");
  176    ApiSystem::executeScriptLegacy("rm /storage/roms/moonlight/*");
  177    ApiSystem::executeScriptLegacy("mkdir -p /storage/roms/moonlight");
  178  
  179    auto xml = GetAppListXml();
  180    if (xml.empty()) return false;
  181  
  182  	pugi::xml_document doc;
  183  	auto result = doc.load_string(xml.c_str());
  184   	if (!result) return false;
  185  
  186    // list api returns the following xml
  187    // <?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-16"?>
  188    // <root protocol_version="0.1" query="applist" status_code="200" status_message="OK">
  189    //   <App>
  190    //     <AppInstallPath>C:\Program Files (x86)\Steam\</AppInstallPath>
  191    //     <AppTitle>Steam</AppTitle>
  192    //     <CmsId>100021711</CmsId>
  193    //     <Distributor>Steam</Distributor>
  194    //     <ID>1088017781</ID>
  195    //     <IsAppCollectorGame>0</IsAppCollectorGame>
  196    //     <IsHdrSupported>1</IsHdrSupported>
  197    //     <MaxControllersForSingleSession>1</MaxControllersForSingleSession>
  198    //     <ShortName>steam</ShortName>
  199    //     <SupportedSOPS>
  200    //       <SOPS>
  201    //         <Height>2160</Height>
  202    //         <RefreshRate>60</RefreshRate>
  203    //         <Width>3840</Width>
  204    //       </SOPS>
  205    //       ...
  206    //     </SupportedSOPS>
  207    //     <UniqueId>20225001</UniqueId>
  208    //     <simulateControllers>0</simulateControllers>
  209    //   </App>
  210    //   ...
  211    // </root>
  212  
  213  	pugi::xml_node root = doc.child("root");
  214    if (!root) return false;
  215  
  216    pugi::xml_document gamelist_doc;
  217    auto gamelist = gamelist_doc.append_child("gameList");
  218  
  219  	for (auto app : root.children())
  220  	{
  221      if (std::string(app.name()) != "App") continue;
  222  
  223      std::string title = app.child("AppTitle").text().get();
  224      std::cout << "MoonlightClient::UpdateMoonlightGames: " << title << std::endl;
  225      auto filename = title;
  226      std::replace(filename.begin(), filename.end(), '/', ' ');
  227  
  228      // Write bash script
  229      if (fileExists("/storage/.config/Moonlight Game Streaming Project/Moonlight.conf") == true) {
  230        std::ofstream app_file("/storage/roms/moonlight/" + filename + ".sh");
  231        app_file << "#!/bin/sh" << std::endl;
  232        app_file << "/usr/bin/controller-layout moonlight \"" << filename + ".sh" << "\" /storage/.config/moonlight/gamecontrollerdb.txt" << std::endl;
  233        app_file << "QT_QPA_PLATFORM=wayland moonlight stream " << server_ip_ << " \"" << title << "\" --quit-after" << std::endl;
  234        app_file.close();
  235      } else {
  236        std::ofstream app_file("/storage/roms/moonlight/" + filename + ".sh");
  237        app_file << "#!/bin/sh" << std::endl;
  238        app_file << "/usr/bin/controller-layout moonlight \"" << filename + ".sh" << "\" /storage/.config/moonlight/gamecontrollerdb.txt" << std::endl;
  239        app_file << "moonlight stream -app \"" << title << "\" -platform sdl " << server_ip_ << std::endl;
  240        app_file.close();
  241      }
  242  
  243      // Write box art image
  244      std::string app_id = app.child("ID").text().get();
  245      if (!app_id.empty()) {
  246        WriteBoxArtFile(app_id, "/storage/roms/moonlight/images/" + filename + ".png");
  247      }
  248  
  249      // Update gamelist
  250      auto game = gamelist.append_child("game");
  251      game.append_child("path").text().set(std::string("./" + filename + ".sh").c_str());
  252      game.append_child("name").text().set(title.c_str());
  253      game.append_child("image").text().set(std::string("./images/" + filename + ".png").c_str());
  254    }
  255  
  256    gamelist_doc.save_file("/storage/roms/moonlight/gamelist.xml");
  257    return true;
  258  }

  262  void GuiMoonlight::show(Window* window)
  263  {
  264  	window->pushGui(new GuiMoonlight(window));
  265  }
  266  
  267  GuiMoonlight::GuiMoonlight(Window* window)
  268   : GuiSettings(window, "MOONLIGHT GAME STREAMING")
  269  {
  270    char pin[5];
  271    snprintf(pin, sizeof pin, "%04d", rand() % 10000);
  272  
  273  	auto theme = ThemeData::getMenuTheme();
  274  	std::shared_ptr<Font> font = theme->Text.font;
  275  	unsigned int color = theme->Text.color;
  276  	auto pinUI = std::make_shared<TextComponent>(window, pin, font, color);
  277  
  278  	addGroup(_("TOOLS"));
  279  
  280    addEntry(_("QUIT CURRENT GAME"), false, [window] {
  281      std::string server_ip = SystemConf::getInstance()->get("moonlight.host");
  282      char cmd[1024];
  283      snprintf(cmd, sizeof cmd, "moonlight quit %s", server_ip.c_str());
  284      ApiSystem::executeScriptLegacy(cmd);
  285    });
  286  
  287    addEntry(_("UPDATE MOONLIGHT GAMES"), false, [window] {
  288      std::string server_ip = SystemConf::getInstance()->get("moonlight.host");
  289      
  290      MoonlightClient client(server_ip);
  291      if (!server_ip.empty() && client.UpdateMoonlightGames()) {
  292        Scripting::fireEvent("quit", "restart");
  293  			Utils::Platform::quitES(Utils::Platform::QuitMode::QUIT);
  294      } else {
  295        window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, _("Unable to connect to server")));
  296      }
  297    });
  298  
  299    addEntry(_("PAIR WITH SERVER"), false, [window, pin] {
  300      std::string server_ip = SystemConf::getInstance()->get("moonlight.host");
  301  
  302      char cmd[1024];
  303      if (isEmbedded() == false) {
  304        snprintf(cmd, sizeof cmd, "QT_QPA_PLATFORM=wayland moonlight pair -pin %s %s", pin, server_ip.c_str());
  305      } else {
  306        snprintf(cmd, sizeof cmd, "moonlight pair -pin %s %s", pin, server_ip.c_str());
  307      }
  308  		ApiSystem::executeScriptLegacy(cmd, [server_ip, window](std::string line) {
  309        std::string new_server_ip;
  310        if (ParseServerIp(line, &new_server_ip) && server_ip != new_server_ip) {
  311    			SystemConf::getInstance()->set("moonlight.host", new_server_ip);
  312        }
  313        const std::string pared_ok = "Succesfully paired";
  314        if (line == "Succesfully paired") {
  315          window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, _("Succesfully paired with server")));
  316        }
  317      if (fileExists("/storage/.config/Moonlight Game Streaming Project/Moonlight.conf") == true) {
  318        const std::string cert_path = "/storage/.cache/Moonlight Game Streaming Project/";
  319        if (fileExists(cert_path + "client.pem") == false) {
  320          extractClient();
  321        }
  322        if (fileExists(cert_path + "key.pem") == false) {
  323          extractKey();
  324        }
  325      }
  326  		});
  327  	});
  328  
  329    addEntry(_("UNPAIR WITH SERVER"), false, [this, window] {
  330  		if (fileExists("/storage/.config/Moonlight Game Streaming Project/Moonlight.conf") == true) {
  331        Utils::Platform::runSystemCommand("rm -r \"~/.cache/Moonlight Game Streaming Project\"", "", nullptr);
  332        Utils::Platform::runSystemCommand("rm -r \"~/.config/Moonlight Game Streaming Project\"", "", nullptr);
  333        window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, _("Unpaired and settings cleared")));
  334      } else {
  335        Utils::Platform::runSystemCommand("rm -r ~/.cache/moonlight", "", nullptr);
  336        window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, _("Unpaired from server")));
  337      }
  338  	});
  339  
  340  	addGroup(_("SETTINGS"));
  341    addInputTextConfigRow(_("SERVER IP"), "moonlight.host", false);
  342    addWithLabel(_("PAIRING PIN"), pinUI);
  343  }
  344  
  345  std::vector<std::string> GuiMoonlight::ParseAppList(const std::vector<std::string>& vec) {
  346    std::vector<std::string> apps;
  347    for (auto line : vec) {
  348      int pos = line.find(". ");
  349      if (pos == -1) continue;
  350      apps.push_back(line.substr(pos+2));
  351    }
  352    return apps;
  353  }
  354  
  355  bool GuiMoonlight::ParseServerIp(const std::string& line, std::string* server_ip) {
  356    // Watching for "Connect to 10.1.10.217..." line.
  357    const std::string prompt = "Connect to ";
  358    if (line.find(prompt) != 0) return false;
  359  
  360    const std::string ip = line.substr(prompt.length());
  361    if (ip.substr(ip.length() - 3) != "...") return false;
  362  
  363    *server_ip = ip.substr(0, ip.length() - 3);
  364    return true;
  365  }

  477

  479  
  480  	pugi::xml_document doc;
  481  
  482  	pugi::xml_node config = doc.append_child("config"); // root element
  483  
  484  	saveMap<std::string, bool>(config, mBoolMap, "bool", mDefaultBoolMap, false);
  485  	saveMap<std::string, int>(config, mIntMap, "int", mDefaultIntMap, 0);
  486  	saveMap<std::string, float>(config, mFloatMap, "float", mDefaultFloatMap, 0);
  487  
  488  	//saveMap<std::string, std::string>(config, mStringMap, "string");
  489  	for(auto iter = mStringMap.cbegin(); iter != mStringMap.cend(); iter++)
  490  	{
  491  		// key is on the "don't save" list, so don't save it
  492  		if (std::find(settings_dont_save.cbegin(), settings_dont_save.cend(), iter->first) != settings_dont_save.cend())
  493  			continue;
  494  
  495  		// Value is not known, and empty, don't save it
  496  		auto def = mDefaultStringMap.find(iter->first);
  497  		if (def == mDefaultStringMap.cend() && iter->second.empty())
  498  			continue;
  499  
  500  		// Value is know and has default value, don't save it
  501  		if (def != mDefaultStringMap.cend() && def->second == iter->second)
  502  			continue;
  503  
  504  		pugi::xml_node node = config.append_child("string");
  505  		node.append_attribute("name").set_value(iter->first.c_str());
  506  		node.append_attribute("value").set_value(iter->second.c_str());
  507  	}
  508  
  509  	// Serialised once, then written through a temporary and a rename
  510  	// (D-CLOUD-079): save_file opened the live path for writing, which
  511  	// truncates it, and a process killed before the write completed -- or a
  512  	// battery gone at that instant -- left an empty es_settings.cfg that the
  513  	// next start read as "no settings" and the next save made permanent.
  514  	// The same bytes go to the last-known-good record, because what was just
  515  	// written is, by construction, the newest good state (D-CLOUD-078).
  516  	struct StringWriter : pugi::xml_writer
  517  	{
  518  		std::string out;
  519  		void write(const void* data, size_t size) override { out.append((const char*) data, size); }
  520  	};
  521  	StringWriter writer;
  522  	doc.save(writer);
  523  
  524  	if (!Utils::AtomicFile::writeText(path, writer.out))
  525  	{
  526  		// Kept dirty, so the next save tries again rather than believing this
  527  		// one happened.
  528  		LOG(LogError) << "Settings::saveFile() : could not write " << path << "; the changes are kept for the next save";
  529  		mWasChanged = true;
  530  		return false;
  531  	}
  532  	// The record is made no less private than the live file (#308 F-ES-08).
  533  	if (!Utils::AtomicFile::writeText(path + ".backup", writer.out, Utils::AtomicFile::modeOf(path, 0644)))
  534  		LOG(LogWarning) << "Settings::saveFile() : could not write the last-known-good record " << path << ".backup";
  535  
  536  	Scripting::fireEvent("config-changed");
  537  	Scripting::fireEvent("settings-changed");
  538  
  539  	return true;
  540  }
  541  
  542  // Read the live file if it parses; else the last-known-good record beside it
  543  // (es_settings.cfg.backup), which then goes back as the live file; else
  544  // nothing -- the defaults stand and nothing is written over the file that
  545  // failed, so a start that found it damaged and a record that was missing
  546  // leaves both for somebody to look at (D-CLOUD-078, D-CLOUD-079). An empty
  547  // file is a parse failure here: pugixml reports no document element, which
  548  // is exactly what a write cut short leaves behind.

  285  bool SystemConf::saveSystemConf()
  286  {

  288  		return Settings::getInstance()->saveFile();

  293  	// The shell's settings lock (wait_lock in profile.d/001-functions), held
  294  	// across the read-modify-write: set_setting reads the file, writes a
  295  	// temporary and renames it under the same lock, and a save that read or
  296  	// renamed beside it put one writer's snapshot over the other's. Five
  297  	// seconds is the budget, on the interface thread. A live holder still on
  298  	// the lock past it is logged and nothing is written (PL-024): this used to
  299  	// save anyway, and lost either the script's key or the player's. The
  300  	// changes stay in changedConf, and the next save -- any page closing,
  301  	// any set-and-save -- writes them over whatever the script left.

  303  	bool baseWhole = true;
  304  	const Utils::AtomicFile::LockedSave saved = Utils::AtomicFile::saveUnderLock(mSystemConfFile, sLockPath, sLockBudgetMs,
  305  		[this](const std::string& current) { return applyChanges(current); }, &out, &baseWhole);
  306  
  307  	switch (saved)
  308  	{
  309  	case Utils::AtomicFile::LockedSave::LockBusy:
  310  		LOG(LogWarning) << "saveSystemConf: the settings lock was not free within 5s; nothing written -- the changes are kept for the next save";
  311  		return false;
  312  	case Utils::AtomicFile::LockedSave::Unreadable:
  313  		LOG(LogError) << "Unable to open for saving :  " << mSystemConfFile << " -- the changes are kept for the next save";
  314  		return false;
  315  	case Utils::AtomicFile::LockedSave::WriteFailed:
  316  		// Written to a temporary of its own, synced, and renamed over the
  317  		// live file (D-CLOUD-079, PL-063); a failure leaves the file whole.
  318  		LOG(LogError) << "Unable to write " << mSystemConfFile << " -- the changes are kept for the next save";
  319  		return false;
  320  	case Utils::AtomicFile::LockedSave::Written:
  321  		break;
  322  	}
  323  
  324  	changedConf.clear();

  439  bool SystemConf::set(const std::string &name, const std::string &value) 
  440  {
  441  	if (mSystemConfFile.empty())
  442  		return Settings::getInstance()->setString(mapSettingsName(name), value == "auto" ? "" : value);
  443  
  444  	if (confMap.count(name) == 0 || confMap[name] != value)
  445  	{
  446  		// What the key held before this run of changes, once: a reload
  447  		// compares it with the file (G-E1-03). Held in the file, as last
  448  		// read or written -- not in confMap, which keeps keys the file no
  449  		// longer has: one another writer removed, one the last save wrote
  450  		// away. The reload found such a key missing where the base said it
  451  		// was, and dropped the change as removed by somebody since (audit of
  452  		// the fix round, gpt G2-E-core-07, claude G2-E-core-09).
  453  		if (changedConf.find(name) == changedConf.cend())
  454  		{
  455  			const auto it = mOnDisk.find(name);
  456  			mPendingBase[name] = it == mOnDisk.cend() ? std::make_pair(false, std::string()) : std::make_pair(true, it->second);
  457  		}
  458  		confMap[name] = value;
  459  		changedConf.insert(name);
  460  		return true;
  461  	}
  462  
  463  	return false;
  464  }

  436  #if defined(_WIN32)
  437  			(void) path;

  439  #else

  443  			int r;

  446  			while (r != 0 && errno == EINTR);
  447  			const bool held = r != 0;   // EWOULDBLOCK: somebody has it; anything else: cannot tell
  448  			::close(fd);                // and the lock, if this took it, with it
  449  			return held;
  450  #endif

  872  std::vector<BiosSystem> ApiSystem::getBiosInformations(const std::string system, bool all) 
  873  {
  874  	std::vector<BiosSystem> res;
  875  	BiosSystem current;
  876  	bool isCurrent = false;
  877  
  878  	// The default output lists problems only, which is what the launch-time
  879  	// check wants. --all adds every present file, for the BIOS CHECK page.
  880  	std::string cmd = "rocknix-systems";
  881  	if (all)
  882  		cmd += " --all";
  883  	else if (!system.empty())
  884  		cmd += " --filter " + system;
  885  
  886  	auto systems = executeEnumerationScript(cmd);
  887  	for (auto line : systems)
  888  	{
  889  		if (Utils::String::startsWith(line, "> ")) 
  890  		{
  891  			if (isCurrent)
  892  				res.push_back(current);
  893  
  894  			isCurrent = true;
  895  			current.name = std::string(std::string(line).substr(2));
  896  			current.bios.clear();
  897  		}
  898  		else 
  899  		{
  900  			BiosFile biosFile;
  901  			std::vector<std::string> tokens = Utils::String::split(line, ' ');
  902  			if (tokens.size() >= 3) 
  903  			{
  904  				biosFile.status = tokens.at(0);
  905  				biosFile.md5 = tokens.at(1);
  906  
  907  				// concatenat the ending words
  908  				std::string vname = "";
  909  				for (unsigned int i = 2; i < tokens.size(); i++) 
  910  				{
  911  					if (i > 2) vname += " ";
  912  					vname += tokens.at(i);

   47  	// every look, which stats the record, so a record written by a session
   48  	// or by hand over ssh is seen on the next look without a restart; the
   49  	// cache used to hold the whole Transform and kept the turn it was built
   50  	// with until the process ended (audit #258 PL-019). A FileData pointer
   51  	// is not cached either: the list that owns it is rebuilt by a rescan
   52  	// (#246's lesson), so the game is found again by path each time.
   53  	// forgetScreenshots empties it when a session records a rotation, which
   54  	// also covers a screenshot whose game arrived after the first look.
   55  	struct ShotGame { std::string system; std::string gamePath; };
   56  	static std::mutex sShotLock;
   57  	static std::map<std::string, ShotGame> sShots;
   58  	// The library by stem, built on the first screenshot that misses sShots
   59  	// and kept until forgetScreenshots: a list of S never-seen screenshots
   60  	// used to walk every game S times on the interface thread (audit #258,
   61  	// the seat's G-04); now the library is walked once. The first game of
   62  	// the first system to carry a stem wins, as the walk did.
   63  	static std::map<std::string, ShotGame> sStems;
   64  	static bool sStemsBuilt = false;
   65  
   66  	void forgetScreenshots()
   67  	{
   68  		std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(sShotLock);
   69  		sShots.clear();
   70  		sStems.clear();
   71  		sStemsBuilt = false;
   72  	}
   73  
   74  	static void buildStems()
   75  	{
   76  		sStems.clear();
   77  		for (SystemData* system : SystemData::sSystemVector)
   78  		{
   79  			if (system == nullptr || system->isCollection() || !system->isGameSystem() || system->getRootFolder() == nullptr)
   80  				continue;
   81  			if (system->hasPlatformId(PlatformIds::IMAGEVIEWER))
   82  				continue;
   83  			for (FileData* game : system->getRootFolder()->getFilesRecursive(GAME))
   84  				sStems.emplace(Utils::FileSystem::getStem(game->getPath()), ShotGame{ system->getName(), game->getPath() });
   85  		}
   86  		sStemsBuilt = true;
   87  	}
   88  
   89  	static FileData* findGame(const ShotGame& sg)
   90  	{
   91  		if (sg.system.empty())
   92  			return nullptr;
   93  		SystemData* system = SystemData::getSystem(sg.system);
   94  		if (system == nullptr || system->getRootFolder() == nullptr)
   95  			return nullptr;
   96  		return system->getRootFolder()->FindByPath(sg.gamePath);
   97  	}
   98  
   99  	Transform forScreenshotPath(const std::string& path)
  100  	{
  101  		{
  102  			std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(sShotLock);
  103  			auto it = sShots.find(path);
  104  			if (it != sShots.cend())
  105  			{
  106  				if (it->second.system.empty())
  107  					return Transform();
  108  				if (FileData* game = findGame(it->second))
  109  					return forGame(game);
  110  				// The list was rebuilt under it: look again below.
  111  			}
  112  		}
  113  		const std::string content = DisplayAspectText::screenshotContent(Utils::FileSystem::getFileName(path));
  114  		Transform t;
  115  		ShotGame sg;
  116  		std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(sShotLock);
  117  		if (!sStemsBuilt)
  118  			buildStems();
  119  		if (!content.empty())
  120  		{
  121  			auto it = sStems.find(content);
  122  			if (it != sStems.cend())
  123  			{
  124  				sg = it->second;
  125  				if (FileData* game = findGame(sg))
  126  					t = forGame(game);
  127  			}
  128  		}
  129  		sShots[path] = sg;
  130  		return t;
  131  	}
  132  
  133  	Transform forScreenshot(FileData* screenshot)
  134  	{

  137  		return forScreenshotPath(screenshot->getPath());
  138  	}
